### Task 12: Callback verifies the signed state and membership before exchanging the code (C4)

**Files:**
- Test: `tests/unit/api/enablebanking/callback.test.ts`
- Modify (full replacement): `app/api/enablebanking/callback/route.ts` (currently lines 1–40)

Behaviour: the bank redirect carries no Bearer token, so the signed `state` is the only proof of who started the flow. Order of checks:
1. `state` missing → `400 { error: 'invalid state' }`.
2. `verifyEbState` fails → 400 (`invalid state` / `expired state`); an empty `EB_STATE_SECRET` returns `500 { error: 'server not configured' }`.
3. `requireOrgMember(uid, orgId)` fails → 403.
4. Only then is the code exchanged through `POST https://api.enablebanking.com/sessions`.

Redirects stay as they were: `307` to `/{orgId}/settings?eb_auth=<base64url {"sessionId"}>` on success and `/{orgId}/settings?eb_error=1` on upstream failure. One addition: a valid `state` without `code` (the user cancelled, so Enable Banking sends `?error=...&error_description=...`) also redirects to `?eb_error=1`. Before, it went to `/`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/api/enablebanking/callback.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { TEST_EB_STATE_SECRET, resetFakeAdmin, upstreamJson } from '@/tests/unit/helpers/fake-admin'
import { EB_STATE_TTL_SECONDS, signEbState } from '@/lib/server/eb-state'
import { GET } from '@/app/api/enablebanking/callback/route'

vi.mock('@/lib/server/firebase-admin', async () => {
  const { fakeAdmin: admin } = await import('@/tests/unit/helpers/fake-admin')
  return { getAdmin: () => admin }
})
vi.mock('@/lib/enablebanking/jwt', () => ({ createJwt: vi.fn(async () => 'eb-jwt') }))

const fetchMock = vi.fn()

function callbackRequest(query: Record<string, string>): Request {
  const url = new URL('http://localhost:3000/api/enablebanking/callback')
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(query)) url.searchParams.set(k, v)
  return new Request(url)
}

function aliceState(nowSec?: number): string {
  return signEbState({ orgId: 'org-acme', uid: 'user-alice' }, TEST_EB_STATE_SECRET, nowSec)
}

describe('GET /api/enablebanking/callback', () => {
  let errorSpy: { mockRestore: () => void }

  beforeEach(() => {
    resetFakeAdmin()
    fetchMock.mockReset()
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
    vi.stubEnv('EB_STATE_SECRET', TEST_EB_STATE_SECRET)
    errorSpy = vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {})
  })

  afterEach(() => {
    errorSpy.mockRestore()
    vi.unstubAllGlobals()
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  })

  it('returns 400 "invalid state" when state is missing', async () => {
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code' }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(400)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'invalid state' })
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('returns 400 for the legacy unsigned base64 state', async () => {
    const legacy = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ orgId: 'org-acme' })).toString('base64url')
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state: legacy }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(400)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'invalid state' })
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('returns 400 for a state forged with another secret, before exchanging the code', async () => {
    const forged = signEbState({ orgId: 'org-acme', uid: 'user-alice' }, 'attacker-secret')
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state: forged }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(400)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'invalid state' })
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('returns 400 "expired state" for a state older than 15 minutes', async () => {
    const issuedAt = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - EB_STATE_TTL_SECONDS - 5
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state: aliceState(issuedAt) }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(400)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'expired state' })
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('returns 403 when the user in the state is no longer a member, before exchanging the code', async () => {
    const state = signEbState({ orgId: 'org-acme', uid: 'user-mallory' }, TEST_EB_STATE_SECRET)
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(403)
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('returns 403 when the org in the state no longer exists', async () => {
    const state = signEbState({ orgId: 'org-ghost', uid: 'user-alice' }, TEST_EB_STATE_SECRET)
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(403)
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('exchanges the code and redirects to /{orgId}/settings?eb_auth=...', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValueOnce(upstreamJson({ session_id: 'sess-123', accounts: [] }))

    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state: aliceState() }))

    expect(res.status).toBe(307)
    const location = new URL(res.headers.get('location')!)
    expect(location.origin).toBe('http://localhost:3000')
    expect(location.pathname).toBe('/org-acme/settings')
    const payload = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(location.searchParams.get('eb_auth')!, 'base64url').toString('utf8'))
    expect(payload).toEqual({ sessionId: 'sess-123' })

    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    const [url, init] = fetchMock.mock.calls[0] as [string, RequestInit]
    expect(url).toBe('https://api.enablebanking.com/sessions')
    expect(init.method).toBe('POST')
    expect((init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization).toBe('Bearer eb-jwt')
    expect(JSON.parse(String(init.body))).toEqual({ code: 'auth-code' })
  })

  it('redirects to settings with eb_error=1 when the bank returns no code (user cancelled)', async () => {
    const res = await GET(
      callbackRequest({ state: aliceState(), error: 'access_denied', error_description: 'Denied data sharing consent' }),
    )
    expect(res.status).toBe(307)
    expect(res.headers.get('location')).toBe('http://localhost:3000/org-acme/settings?eb_error=1')
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('redirects to settings with eb_error=1 when the code exchange fails', async () => {
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValueOnce(upstreamJson({ message: 'invalid code' }, 400))
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state: aliceState() }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(307)
    expect(res.headers.get('location')).toBe('http://localhost:3000/org-acme/settings?eb_error=1')
  })

  it('returns 500 "server not configured" when EB_STATE_SECRET is empty', async () => {
    const state = aliceState()
    vi.stubEnv('EB_STATE_SECRET', '')
    const res = await GET(callbackRequest({ code: 'auth-code', state }))
    expect(res.status).toBe(500)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'server not configured' })
    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `app/api/enablebanking/callback/route.ts` (whole file)**

```ts
import { createJwt } from '@/lib/enablebanking/jwt'
import { requireOrgMember } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { verifyEbState } from '@/lib/server/eb-state'
import { errorResponse, HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'

const BASE = 'https://api.enablebanking.com'

function redirectTo(req: Request, path: string): Response {
  return new Response(null, { status: 307, headers: { Location: new URL(path, req.url).toString() } })
}

// GET /api/enablebanking/callback?code=xxx&state=xxx
// Enable Banking redirects the browser here after the bank login, so there is no Bearer token:
// the HMAC-signed `state` created by /api/enablebanking/session identifies the org and the user.
// The state and the user's membership are verified BEFORE the code is exchanged.
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const params = new URL(req.url).searchParams
  const code = params.get('code')
  const state = params.get('state')

  let orgId: string
  try {
    if (!state) throw new HttpError(400, 'invalid state')
    const verified = verifyEbState(state, process.env.EB_STATE_SECRET!)
    await requireOrgMember(verified.uid, verified.orgId)
    orgId = verified.orgId
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(e)
  }

  const settingsPath = `/${encodeURIComponent(orgId)}/settings`

  // No code: the user cancelled at the bank or Enable Banking returned ?error=...
  if (!code) {
    console.error('Enable Banking callback without code:', params.get('error'), params.get('error_description'))
    return redirectTo(req, `${settingsPath}?eb_error=1`)
  }

  try {
    const token = await createJwt()
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/sessions`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ code }),
    })

    if (!res.ok) {
      console.error('Enable Banking session error:', res.status, await res.text())
      return redirectTo(req, `${settingsPath}?eb_error=1`)
    }

    const data = await res.json()
    const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ sessionId: data.session_id })).toString('base64url')
    return redirectTo(req, `${settingsPath}?eb_auth=${payload}`)
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Callback error:', e)
    return redirectTo(req, `${settingsPath}?eb_error=1`)
  }
}
```

### Task 13: `ebFetch` client helper, used by `EnableBankingConnect` (C5)

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/enablebanking/client.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/enablebanking/client.test.ts`
- Modify: `components/settings/EnableBankingConnect.tsx`, at the import block (lines 9–14), the institutions call in `loadInstitutions` (lines 79–81) and the session call in `handleConnect` (lines 99–105). Line numbers refer to commit `b68ecca`; if they have shifted, match the snippet text.

`ebFetch` waits for `auth.authStateReady()` (Firebase JS SDK `Auth.authStateReady(): Promise<void>`, https://firebase.google.com/docs/reference/js/auth.auth) so that a call made right after page load does not see `currentUser === null`. It throws `Error('not signed in')` when nobody is signed in. To keep the ID token from leaking to another origin, it refuses any path that does not start with `/api/enablebanking/`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/enablebanking/client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { ebFetch } from '@/lib/enablebanking/client'

const fakeAuth = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  authStateReady: vi.fn(async () => {}),
  currentUser: null as null | { getIdToken: () => Promise<string> },
}))
vi.mock('@/lib/firebase/firebase', () => ({ auth: fakeAuth }))

const fetchMock = vi.fn()

describe('ebFetch', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    fakeAuth.authStateReady.mockClear()
    fakeAuth.currentUser = { getIdToken: vi.fn(async () => 'id-token-1') }
    fetchMock.mockReset()
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(new Response('{}', { status: 200 }))
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
  })

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllGlobals()
  })

  it('adds Authorization: Bearer <ID token> and keeps method, body and other headers', async () => {
    await ebFetch('/api/enablebanking/session', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)

    expect(init.body).toBe('{"orgId":"org-acme"}')
    const headers = new Headers(init.headers)
    expect(headers.get('authorization')).toBe('Bearer id-token-1')
    expect(headers.get('content-type')).toBe('application/json')

    expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled()

    await expect(ebFetch('/api/other')).rejects.toThrow('ebFetch: only /api/enablebanking/* paths are allowed')
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `lib/enablebanking/client.ts`**

```ts
'use client'

import { auth } from '@/lib/firebase/firebase'

const ALLOWED_PREFIX = '/api/enablebanking/'

/**
 * fetch() for FinFlow's own /api/enablebanking/* routes from the browser.
 * Adds `Authorization: Bearer <Firebase ID token>` of the signed-in user.
 * getIdToken() returns the cached token and refreshes it only when it is about to expire.
 */
export async function ebFetch(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  if (!path.startsWith(ALLOWED_PREFIX)) {
    throw new Error('ebFetch: only /api/enablebanking/* paths are allowed')
  }
  await auth.authStateReady()
  const user = auth.currentUser
  if (!user) throw new Error('not signed in')
  const token = await user.getIdToken()
  const headers = new Headers(init?.headers)
  headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`)
  return fetch(path, { ...init, headers })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the Part C probe runner (it starts and stops its own dev server)**

An agent has no second terminal, and a dev server left running would keep a port busy for Playwright later (`reuseExistingServer: false` fails with `http://localhost:3000 is already used`). So, like `/tmp/finflow-smoke.sh` in Part A, one Node script starts `next dev` on port **3102** (it reads `.env.local` exactly like `npm run dev`), runs every probe, kills the server's process tree and checks that the port is free again. It signs the expired-state probe with the local `EB_STATE_SECRET` and never prints the value. It uses `fetch` from a script file, which the local context-mode hook does not intercept.

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
cat > /tmp/finflow-c6.mjs <<'EOF'
// FinFlow Part C probe runner (S00 Task 14). Run from the repo root:
//   node /tmp/finflow-c6.mjs
// Starts `next dev` on :3102 (it reads .env.local like `npm run dev`), probes the routes
// without credentials, probes the callback with an expired state signed with the local
// EB_STATE_SECRET (the value is never printed), then kills the server's process tree.
import { execSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process'
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto'
import { existsSync, openSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { createServer } from 'node:net'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'

const port = 3102
const base = `http://localhost:${port}`
const logFile = join(tmpdir(), 'finflow-c6-dev.log')
const out = openSync(logFile, 'w')
const server = spawn(process.execPath, ['node_modules/next/dist/bin/next', 'dev', '-p', String(port)], {
  env: { ...process.env, NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: '1' },
  detached: process.platform !== 'win32',
  stdio: ['ignore', out, out],
})
const stop = () => {
  try {
    if (process.platform === 'win32') execSync(`taskkill /PID ${server.pid} /T /F`, { stdio: 'ignore' })
    else process.kill(-server.pid, 'SIGTERM')
  } catch {}
}
const portFree = () =>
  new Promise((resolve) => {
    const s = createServer()
    s.once('error', () => resolve(false))
    s.once('listening', () => s.close(() => resolve(true)))
    s.listen(port)
  })

// An already-expired state (e: 1) signed with the local secret; only the HTTP answer is printed.
function expiredState() {
  const env = existsSync('.env.local') ? readFileSync('.env.local', 'utf8') : ''
  const m = env.match(/^EB_STATE_SECRET=(.+)$/m)
  if (!m) return null
  const p = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ o: 'org-x', u: 'user-x', n: '0011223344556677', e: 1 })).toString('base64url')
  return `${p}.${createHmac('sha256', m[1].trim()).update(p).digest('base64url')}`
}

let failed = 0
const check = (name, ok, detail) => {
  if (!ok) failed++
  console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${name}${ok ? '' : ' :: ' + detail}`)
}
try {
  for (let i = 0; ; i++) {
    try { await fetch(`${base}/api/health`, { redirect: 'manual' }); break } catch { if (i >= 120) throw new Error('next dev did not start in 120 s') }
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000))
  }
  const expired = expiredState()
  if (!expired) check('EB_STATE_SECRET present in .env.local', false, 'missing: run Task C3 Step 1')
  const json = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
  const probes = [
    ['GET /api/test/auth -> 404', '/api/test/auth', {}, 404],
    ['GET /test-login -> 404', '/test-login', {}, 404],
    ['POST session without token -> 401', '/api/enablebanking/session', { method: 'POST', headers: json, body: JSON.stringify({ aspspName: 'Bank Millennium', orgId: 'x' }) }, 401, 'unauthorized'],
    ['GET institutions without token -> 401', '/api/enablebanking/institutions?country=PL', {}, 401, 'unauthorized'],
    ['GET accounts without token -> 401', '/api/enablebanking/accounts?sessionId=s1&orgId=x', {}, 401, 'unauthorized'],
    ['GET callback with a forged state -> 400 invalid state', '/api/enablebanking/callback?code=abc&state=Zm9v.YmFy', {}, 400, 'invalid state'],
    ...(expired ? [['GET callback with an expired state -> 400 expired state', `/api/enablebanking/callback?code=abc&state=${expired}`, {}, 400, 'expired state']] : []),
  ]
  for (const [name, path, init, status, error] of probes) {
    const r = await fetch(base + path, { redirect: 'manual', ...init })
    const body = await r.text()
    let got = null
    try { got = JSON.parse(body).error } catch {}
    check(name, r.status === status && (error === undefined || got === error), `${r.status} ${body.slice(0, 120)}`)
  }
} catch (e) {
  failed++
  console.log('FAIL', e.message)
} finally {
  stop()
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500))
  check(`port ${port} free after stop`, await portFree(), 'next dev is still running')
  console.log(failed === 0 ? 'C6 PROBES OK' : `C6 PROBES FAILED (${failed}) - server log: ${logFile}`)
  process.exit(failed === 0 ? 0 : 1)
}
EOF
echo written
```

Expected: `written`. Port 3102 must be free.

Second, no earlier task puts `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT`, `ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID` or `ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY` into `.env.local`: Task A2 leaves the Enable Banking variables out on purpose, and the Application ID only arrives in Task E4 Step 8 (`.secrets/enablebanking-app-id.txt`). import { join } from 'node:path'

```sh
    ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID: readFileSync('.secrets/enablebanking-app-id.txt', 'utf8').trim(),
    ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY: readFileSync('.secrets/enablebanking-private.key', 'utf8').replace(/\r/g, ''),

})
child.on('exit', (code) => process.exit(code ?? 0))
EOF
```

Expected: `port 3000 free` and `port 3102 free` (a `BUSY` line means a dev server is still running: find its PID with `netstat -ano | grep -E ':(3000|3102) .*LISTENING'` and stop it with `taskkill //PID <pid> //T //F`), then `git status --short` prints nothing (`.env.local` stays ignored). The log shows the five Part C commits on top: `feat(settings): send Firebase ID token…`, `fix(security): verify signed state…`, `fix(security): require Firebase ID token…`, `feat(server): HMAC-signed Enable Banking OAuth state` and `chore(security): remove test-login backdoor…`.

## Part D: Firestore/Storage rules, indexes, firebase.json, rules tests, emulator wiring

This part puts Firestore access under version-controlled, tested security rules. It adds `firebase.json` and `.firebaserc`, a deny-all `storage.rules`, a member-scoped `firestore.rules`, and composite indexes for the transaction journal. It also adds an emulator test suite (`npm run test:rules`), a black-box REST verifier (`scripts/verify-firestore-rules.mjs`), and Auth/Firestore emulator wiring in the client SDK (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1`) for the CI e2e run. **Preconditions:** Part A Tasks A1–A3 are committed on `s00-platform` (Next 16, `npm ci` done, `lint`/`typecheck` scripts green) and Part B Task B1 is done (vitest 5, `vitest.config.ts`, `tests/unit/server-only-stub.ts`). D1–D5 commit to `s00-platform` and touch production only through read-only `--dry-run` compiles. **D6 (production deploy) runs only after Part E Task E5 has the merged `master` live on Vercel production**, so the production app and the rules switch together. D6 also needs `firebase-admin`, which Part B Task B1 installs.

### Conventions for this part

- Shell: Git Bash. The cwd is always the repo root `F:/AI/finflow` (in Git Bash: `/f/AI/finflow`). Every block starts with `cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1`, and blocks that write, install, deploy or commit also run the repo check from **How to read this plan**. Create and edit files with absolute paths (`F:/AI/finflow/...`).
- Firebase CLI 15.20.0 (global). Production commands always pass `--project finflow-e94b5 --account "$FIREBASE_ACCOUNT"`, where the block first reads `FIREBASE_ACCOUNT` from the gitignored `.env` (Task D1 Step 2 adds it). The account address is never written into the repo, because the repo is public. Emulator commands always pass `--project demo-finflow`.
- **Java.** The emulators need Java 21, and none is installed on this machine. Steps that need it are tagged **(Java)**. They are **optional locally**: Task D1 Step 1 asks the user before installing a portable JRE under `$HOME/.jre/temurin-21` (no admin rights needed). If the user agrees, each **(Java)** command runs with the prefix `PATH="$HOME/.jre/temurin-21/bin:$PATH"`. The PATH entry has to be the POSIX form (`/c/Users/...`, which `$HOME` already is). With a Windows-form entry (`C:/Users/...`), firebase-tools reports ``Could not spawn `java -version` ``. This was verified on this host. If Java comes from a `winget` install and `java -version` works in a fresh shell, drop the prefix. **Without local Java, skip every (Java) step.** CI verifies them instead: Task E3 Step 6 pushes `s00-platform` as a draft PR right after the CI workflow exists, and the `ci` job runs `test:rules` (the green state of D3 and D5) and the emulator e2e. Two (Java) checks have no CI equivalent and are then simply not run: the Storage emulator compile of the deny-all `storage.rules` (D1 Step 8; the file is not deployed while no bucket exists, D6 Step 6) and the "red" runs (D3 Steps 5 and 8, D5 Step 6). The verifier self-test (D4 Step 5) is replaced by its production run in D6 Step 9, which aborts before any write if the rules are not live.
- The local context-mode hook intercepts `curl` writing to stdout and inline `node -e "fetch(...)"`. In this part, `curl` always writes with `-o <file>`, which is verified to pass the hook. Every Node snippet that needs network access is a script file or a `node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'` heredoc.
- Never print secret values. The public Firebase web config is fetched into the gitignored `.secrets/firebase-web-sdkconfig.json`. The service account JSON is the single file matching `.secrets/finflow-e94b5-firebase-adminsdk-*.json` (the suffix changes whenever the key is regenerated), and it is only ever passed by path.

### Facts verified while writing this part (2026-10-22)

Everything in D1–D5 was executed in a scratch copy of the repo layout: firebase-tools 15.20.0, Firestore emulator 1.21.0, Temurin 21.0.12 JRE, firebase 12.11.0, @firebase/rules-unit-testing 5.0.2, vitest 5.0.5, TypeScript 5.9.3, and `eslint-config-next` 16.2.2 with the repo's `eslint.config.mjs`. The results:

- `npm run test:rules`: 39/39 pass.
- With an allow-all ruleset, 27 of the 36 rules tests fail, which proves the negative tests bite.
- The unit test, `tsc --noEmit` and eslint are all clean.
- `firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,firestore:indexes --dry-run` compiles on the production compiler.
- The REST verifier passes on the emulators. With open rules it aborts before any write.

Production facts, all gathered read-only:

1. **The production rules today are expired test-mode rules.** Ruleset `8ecea49c-f0f7-4858-9eae-7704a8702db3` (released 2026-04-07) is `match /{document=**} { allow read, write: if request.time < timestamp.date(2026, 5, 7); }`. Since 2026-05-07, **every client read and write is denied**. The unauthenticated REST list already returns `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`, and the live client app cannot read Firestore at all. D6 therefore *restores* member-scoped access rather than tightening open rules. Until D6 runs, production checks other than `/api/health` (admin SDK, bypasses rules) cannot pass. Rolling back to this ruleset is fail-closed.
2. **Production data shape** (admin SDK, counts only): 87 `organizations`. Every one has a `members` map of `{ <uid>: { role: <string> } }`, `ownerId` among the members, `plan: 'trial'`, and exactly the top-level keys `createdAt, currency, members, name, ownerId, plan, trialEndsAt`. Subcollections present: `categories` (in 58 orgs), `accounts` (1), `transactions` (1), `bankConnections` (1). The only root collection is `organizations`. `_meta/health` does not exist yet.
3. **No default Storage bucket.** Unauthenticated list on both `finflow-e94b5.firebasestorage.app` and `finflow-e94b5.appspot.com` returns 404. `firebase deploy --only storage` resolves the default bucket in `prepare`. On a 404 it throws `Firebase Storage has not been set up on project 'finflow-e94b5'` and aborts the whole deploy before anything is released (firebase-tools `lib/gcp/storage.js` `getDefaultBucket`, `lib/deploy/index.js` prepare chain). D6 therefore includes `storage` only if a bucket exists.
4. **Production composite indexes: none.** `firebase firestore:indexes` prints `{"indexes": [], "fieldOverrides": []}`, so today the combined journal filters fail with "requires an index".
5. **Dependency versions.**
   - `@firebase/rules-unit-testing@5.0.2` is the newest release with `peerDependencies.firebase: ^12.0.0`. 6.0.0 needs firebase ^13, and the repo stays on 12.
   - vitest 5 has the peers `vite` (required) and `@types/node ^22 || >=24`. Against `@types/node@^20` it fails with `ERESOLVE`, which is why Part B bumps `@types/node` to ^24.
6. **`instanceof FirestoreError` is unreliable under Vitest.** The error thrown through the client module graph was not an instance of the imported class, so `lib/firebase/firestore.ts` checks `code === 'permission-denied'` structurally.
7. **The Storage emulator reports `storage.rules` syntax errors as warnings and still exits 0.** The D1 check therefore greps its output for `storage.rules:`.
8. **firebase-tools refuses any `firestore` deploy or emulator run when `firebase.json` names a missing file** (`Error: File ...firestore.indexes.json does not exist`). D1 therefore creates placeholder `firestore.rules` and `firestore.indexes.json` files.

### Task 16: `firestore.rules`, member-scoped and deny-by-default (D2)

**Files:**
- Modify: `firestore.rules` (replace the whole D1 placeholder, lines 1–10)

The rules are exercised red → green in D3, which has the test harness. This task writes them and compiles them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Read how Firestore evaluates `list` rules against the client query (decision record, no command)**

- **`get` is evaluated against the stored document.** From [Securely query data](https://firebase.google.com/docs/firestore/security/rules-query): "When you use a document ID to retrieve a single document, Rules reads the document and evaluates the request using your security rules and the actual document properties." For a missing document, `resource` is `null` and `resource.data.members` is an error, so `get` is **denied**, not answered with "does not exist". Task D5 adapts `getOrganization` to this.
- **`list` (every query and every `onSnapshot` on a query) is not evaluated document by document.** "Security rules are not filters—queries are all or nothing … Rules evaluates a query against its potential result set instead of the actual field values for all of your documents. If a query could potentially return documents that the client does not have permission to read, the entire request fails." A rule that mentions `resource.data.X` therefore passes only if the query's own constraints guarantee the condition for every possible result.
- **The client query is `where('members.<uid>.role', '!=', null)`** (`lib/firebase/firestore.ts:56-59`). From [Perform simple and compound queries](https://firebase.google.com/docs/firestore/query-data/queries): "Not-equal (!=) and not-in queries exclude documents where the given field does not exist" and "null field values do not match != clauses". Every possible result therefore has `members.<uid>.role` present and non-null. That implies `members[<uid>]` exists, so the rules engine can prove membership. On the wire, the JS SDK sends `!= null` as the unary filter `IS_NOT_NULL`, which the D4 verifier uses over REST.
- **Empirical check** (Firestore emulator 1.21.0, caller `alice`, `orgA` with `members: {alice: …}`, `orgB` with `members: {bob: …}`). Every rule below is prefixed with `request.auth != null &&`:

| `list` rule condition | `role != null` (client today) | `role == 'owner'` | `role in [...]` | unfiltered | `members.bob.role != null` | `members.<uid> != null` | `orderBy(members.<uid>.role)` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `request.auth.uid in resource.data.members` | allowed (orgA) | allowed | allowed | denied | denied | allowed | denied |
| `resource.data.members[request.auth.uid] != null` | **allowed (orgA)** | allowed | allowed | denied | denied | allowed | denied |
| `resource.data.members[request.auth.uid].role != null` ← **chosen** | **allowed (orgA)** | allowed | allowed | denied | denied | denied | denied |
| `….role in ['owner','admin','member']` | denied | allowed | allowed | denied | denied | denied | denied |
| `….role == 'owner'` | denied | allowed | denied | denied | denied | denied | denied |

**Answer to the design question:** yes. A `!=` filter on the map field `members.<uid>.role` satisfies a `list` rule that checks `resource.data.members[request.auth.uid] != null` (row 2). So the client query in `lib/firebase/firestore.ts` stays as it is. D3 only moves it into a shared builder (`lib/firebase/queries.ts`) so the rules test runs the exact client query. The chosen rule (row 3) accepts exactly the queries constrained to the caller's own `members.<uid>.role`, as the spec requires, and rejects unfiltered queries and queries on another uid. D6 re-checks the same query against production over REST.

**Beyond the spec bullets**, both checked against the production data (fact 2):
- `create` also requires `keys().hasOnly([...7 keys])`, the exact keys every one of the 87 production orgs has, and `trialEndsAt <= request.time + 15 days` (14-day trial + 1 day clock skew). Without the latter, a client could self-grant a long trial at creation. That is the same abuse the `update` rule blocks.
- Subcollections are an allow-list (`accounts`, `categories`, `transactions`, `bankConnections`), which are exactly the four present in production, rather than any `{collection}`. Deeper nesting is denied. A new subcollection needs a rules change (S02 rewrites the model anyway).
- Each subcollection request costs one extra billed document read: the `get()` of the parent org.

- [ ] **Step 2: Replace `firestore.rules` with the real rules (full file)**

```
rules_version = '2';

// FinFlow Firestore security rules (S00).
//
// Deny by default: any path that no `match` block below allows is closed to
// web/mobile clients. Server code (firebase-admin in lib/server) bypasses these
// rules entirely.
//
// Data shape (lib/firebase/firestore.ts#createOrganization):
//   organizations/{orgId} {
//     name, currency, ownerId, plan, trialEndsAt, createdAt,
//     members: { <uid>: { role: 'owner' } }
//   }
//   organizations/{orgId}/{accounts|categories|transactions|bankConnections}/{docId}
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    // True when the caller's uid is a key of the org's `members` map.
    function isMemberOf(org) {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid in org.members;
    }

    function orgData(orgId) {
      return get(/databases/$(database)/documents/organizations/$(orgId)).data;
    }

    // Exactly what createOrganization writes: the caller is the only member and
    // the owner, the plan is a trial of at most 14 days (+1 day clock skew).
    function isValidNewOrg() {
      let org = request.resource.data;
      return isSignedIn()
        && org.keys().hasOnly(['name', 'currency', 'ownerId', 'plan', 'trialEndsAt', 'createdAt', 'members'])
        && org.ownerId == request.auth.uid
        && org.members is map
        && org.members.keys().hasOnly([request.auth.uid])
        && org.members[request.auth.uid] == {'role': 'owner'}
        && org.plan == 'trial'
        && org.trialEndsAt is timestamp
        && org.trialEndsAt <= request.time + duration.value(15, 'd');
    }

    match /organizations/{orgId} {
      allow get: if isMemberOf(resource.data);

      // Rules are not filters: a query is allowed only if it can never return
      // an org the caller is not a member of. The client query
      // where(`members.${uid}.role`, '!=', null) (lib/firebase/queries.ts)
      // satisfies this; an unfiltered query or one on another uid is denied.
      allow list: if isSignedIn() && resource.data.members[request.auth.uid].role != null;

      allow create: if isValidNewOrg();

      // Members may edit name/currency etc., but never membership, ownership
      // or billing fields (no privilege escalation, no self-extended trial).
      allow update: if isMemberOf(resource.data)
        && !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys()
              .hasAny(['members', 'ownerId', 'plan', 'trialEndsAt']);

      allow delete: if false;

      match /{subcollection}/{docId} {
        allow read, write: if subcollection in ['accounts', 'categories', 'transactions', 'bankConnections']
          && isMemberOf(orgData(orgId));
      }
    }

    // Server-only documents (e.g. _meta/health read by GET /api/health).
    match /_meta/{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `vitest.rules.config.ts`**

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config'

const root = import.meta.dirname

// Firestore security-rules tests. Run through `npm run test:rules`, which starts
// the Firestore emulator (firebase emulators:exec) and sets FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST.
export default defineConfig({
  resolve: {
    // Same as tsconfig "paths": "@/*" -> "./*"
    alias: { '@': root },
  },
  test: {
    environment: 'node',
    include: ['tests/rules/**/*.test.ts'],
    // All test files share one emulator database, so they must not run in parallel.
    fileParallelism: false,
    testTimeout: 20_000,
    hookTimeout: 30_000,
  },
})
```

`initializeTestEnvironment` finds the emulator through `FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST`, which `firebase emulators:exec` sets. No host or port is hard-coded.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the failing rules test `tests/rules/firestore.rules.test.ts` (full file)**

It imports `userOrganizationsQuery` from `@/lib/firebase/queries`, which does not exist yet. That missing module is the RED state. Once the builder exists, the test runs the exact query that `useOrg`, login and register use.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  assertFails,
  assertSucceeds,
  initializeTestEnvironment,
  type RulesTestContext,
  type RulesTestEnvironment,
} from '@firebase/rules-unit-testing'
import {
  Timestamp,
  addDoc,
  collection,
  deleteDoc,
  doc,
  getDoc,
  getDocs,
  orderBy,
  query,
  setDoc,
  updateDoc,
  where,
  type Firestore,
} from 'firebase/firestore'
import { userOrganizationsQuery } from '@/lib/firebase/queries'

const PROJECT_ID = 'demo-finflow'
// FIRESTORE_RULES_FILE lets the plan's "red" check run this suite against a
// deliberately open ruleset; normally the repo's firestore.rules is used.
const RULES_FILE = process.env.FIRESTORE_RULES_FILE ?? resolve(process.cwd(), 'firestore.rules')
const SUBCOLLECTIONS = ['accounts', 'categories', 'transactions', 'bankConnections'] as const

let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment

// RulesTestContext.firestore() is typed as the compat SDK; the modular API
// accepts that instance at runtime, so narrow the type once here.
function fs(ctx: RulesTestContext): Firestore {
  return ctx.firestore() as unknown as Firestore
}
const anon = () => fs(testEnv.unauthenticatedContext())
const alice = () => fs(testEnv.authenticatedContext('alice'))

// Same shape as lib/firebase/firestore.ts#createOrganization.
function newOrg(uid: string, overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const trialEndsAt = new Date()
  trialEndsAt.setDate(trialEndsAt.getDate() + 14)
  return {
    name: `${uid} org`,
    currency: 'UAH',
    ownerId: uid,
    plan: 'trial',
    trialEndsAt: Timestamp.fromDate(trialEndsAt),
    createdAt: Timestamp.fromDate(new Date()),
    members: { [uid]: { role: 'owner' } },
    ...overrides,
  }
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
    projectId: PROJECT_ID,
    firestore: { rules: readFileSync(RULES_FILE, 'utf8') },
  })
})

afterAll(async () => {
  await testEnv?.cleanup()
})

// Fixture: alice owns orgA, bob owns orgB; each org has one doc per subcollection.
beforeEach(async () => {
  await testEnv.clearFirestore()
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (ctx) => {
    const db = fs(ctx)
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'organizations/orgA'), newOrg('alice'))
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'organizations/orgB'), newOrg('bob'))
    for (const orgId of ['orgA', 'orgB']) {
      for (const sub of SUBCOLLECTIONS) {
        await setDoc(doc(db, `organizations/${orgId}/${sub}/seed`), { orgId, name: 'seed' })
      }
    }
    await setDoc(doc(db, '_meta/health'), { ok: true })
  })
})

describe('anonymous user', () => {
  it('cannot get an organization', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(anon(), 'organizations/orgA')))
  })

  it('cannot list organizations, filtered or not', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDocs(collection(anon(), 'organizations')))
    await assertFails(getDocs(userOrganizationsQuery(anon(), 'alice')))
  })

  it('cannot create, update or delete an organization', async () => {
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(anon(), 'organizations/new'), newOrg('alice')))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(anon(), 'organizations/orgA'), { name: 'x' }))
    await assertFails(deleteDoc(doc(anon(), 'organizations/orgA')))
  })

  it.each(SUBCOLLECTIONS)('cannot read or write %s', async (sub) => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(anon(), `organizations/orgA/${sub}/seed`)))
    await assertFails(getDocs(collection(anon(), `organizations/orgA/${sub}`)))
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(anon(), `organizations/orgA/${sub}/new`), { orgId: 'orgA' }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(anon(), `organizations/orgA/${sub}/seed`), { name: 'x' }))
    await assertFails(deleteDoc(doc(anon(), `organizations/orgA/${sub}/seed`)))
  })

  it('cannot read or write _meta', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(anon(), '_meta/health')))
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(anon(), '_meta/health'), { ok: false }))
  })
})

describe('user A (alice) against org B (owned by bob)', () => {
  it('cannot get org B', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgB')))
  })

  it('cannot list organizations unfiltered or filtered on another uid', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDocs(collection(alice(), 'organizations')))
    await assertFails(getDocs(userOrganizationsQuery(alice(), 'bob')))
  })

  it('cannot update or delete org B', async () => {
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgB'), { name: 'pwned' }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgB'), { 'members.alice': { role: 'owner' } }))
    await assertFails(deleteDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgB')))
  })

  it.each(SUBCOLLECTIONS)('cannot read or write org B %s', async (sub) => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(alice(), `organizations/orgB/${sub}/seed`)))
    await assertFails(getDocs(collection(alice(), `organizations/orgB/${sub}`)))
    await assertFails(addDoc(collection(alice(), `organizations/orgB/${sub}`), { orgId: 'orgB' }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), `organizations/orgB/${sub}/seed`), { name: 'x' }))
    await assertFails(deleteDoc(doc(alice(), `organizations/orgB/${sub}/seed`)))
  })
})

describe('organization create', () => {
  it('allows the exact document createOrganization writes', async () => {
    await assertSucceeds(addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice')))
  })

  it('rejects a foreign ownerId', async () => {
    await assertFails(addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { ownerId: 'bob' })))
  })

  it('rejects extra members', async () => {
    const members = { alice: { role: 'owner' }, bob: { role: 'owner' } }
    await assertFails(addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { members })))
  })

  it('rejects a members map without the creator as owner', async () => {
    await assertFails(
      addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { members: { bob: { role: 'owner' } } })),
    )
    await assertFails(
      addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { members: { alice: { role: 'admin' } } })),
    )
  })

  it('rejects a non-trial plan', async () => {
    await assertFails(addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { plan: 'pro' })))
  })

  it('rejects a trial longer than 15 days', async () => {
    const trialEndsAt = Timestamp.fromDate(new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))
    await assertFails(addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { trialEndsAt })))
  })

  it('rejects unknown top-level fields', async () => {
    await assertFails(
      addDoc(collection(alice(), 'organizations'), newOrg('alice', { stripeCustomerId: 'cus_x' })),
    )
  })
})

describe('organization read, update and delete by its member', () => {
  it('member can get own org', async () => {
    await assertSucceeds(getDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA')))
  })

  it('member can list own orgs with the client query and sees only them', async () => {
    const snap = await assertSucceeds(getDocs(userOrganizationsQuery(alice(), 'alice')))
    expect(snap.docs.map((d) => d.id)).toEqual(['orgA'])
  })

  it('member can change name and currency (settings page)', async () => {
    await assertSucceeds(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { name: 'Renamed', currency: 'PLN' }))
  })

  it('member cannot change members', async () => {
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { 'members.bob': { role: 'owner' } }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { members: { alice: { role: 'admin' } } }))
  })

  it('member cannot change plan, trialEndsAt or ownerId', async () => {
    const later = Timestamp.fromDate(new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { plan: 'pro' }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { trialEndsAt: later }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { ownerId: 'bob' }))
    await assertFails(updateDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA'), { name: 'ok', plan: 'pro' }))
  })

  it('member cannot delete own org', async () => {
    await assertFails(deleteDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA')))
  })
})

describe('owner subcollections', () => {
  it.each(SUBCOLLECTIONS)('owner can create, get, list, update and delete %s', async (sub) => {
    const db = alice()
    const ref = doc(db, `organizations/orgA/${sub}/item1`)
    await assertSucceeds(setDoc(ref, { orgId: 'orgA', name: 'item' }))
    await assertSucceeds(addDoc(collection(db, `organizations/orgA/${sub}`), { orgId: 'orgA', name: 'auto-id' }))
    await assertSucceeds(getDoc(ref))
    await assertSucceeds(getDocs(collection(db, `organizations/orgA/${sub}`)))
    await assertSucceeds(updateDoc(ref, { name: 'changed' }))
    await assertSucceeds(deleteDoc(ref))
  })

  it('owner can run the transactions journal query with combined filters', async () => {
    const q = query(
      collection(alice(), 'organizations/orgA/transactions'),
      orderBy('date', 'desc'),
      where('accountId', '==', 'acc1'),
      where('categoryId', '==', 'cat1'),
      where('date', '>=', Timestamp.fromDate(new Date('2026-01-01'))),
    )
    await assertSucceeds(getDocs(q))
  })

  it('owner cannot use subcollections outside the allow-list or nested deeper', async () => {
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA/secrets/x'), { a: 1 }))
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA/secrets/x')))
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(alice(), 'organizations/orgA/accounts/seed/history/h1'), { a: 1 }))
  })
})

describe('server-only and unknown paths', () => {
  it('signed-in user cannot read or write _meta', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(alice(), '_meta/health')))
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(alice(), '_meta/health'), { ok: false }))
  })

  it('everything else is denied by default', async () => {
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(alice(), 'users/alice')))
    await assertFails(setDoc(doc(alice(), 'users/alice'), { a: 1 }))
    await assertFails(getDocs(collection(alice(), 'anything')))
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `lib/firebase/queries.ts` (full file)**

It must not import `./firebase`, so the rules test can pass its own Firestore instance.

```ts
import { collection, query, where, type Firestore, type Query } from 'firebase/firestore'

/**
 * Organizations the user is a member of.
 *
 * firestore.rules only allows `list` on /organizations when the query is
 * constrained to the caller's own `members.<uid>.role` (rules are not filters),
 * so every org listing in the client must go through this builder. The rules
 * test (tests/rules/firestore.rules.test.ts) runs this exact query.
 */
export function userOrganizationsQuery(db: Firestore, uid: string): Query {
  return query(collection(db, 'organizations'), where(`members.${uid}.role`, '!=', null))
}
```

### Task 18: Composite indexes for the journal filters and a black-box REST verifier (D4)

**Files:**
- Modify: `firestore.indexes.json` (replace the D1 skeleton, lines 1–4)
- Create: `scripts/verify-firestore-rules.mjs`

- [ ] **Step 1: Map every query in the code to its index (decision record, no command)**

The only compound query in the client is `getTransactions` (`lib/firebase/transactions.ts:64-77`):

```ts
let q: Query = query(txsRef(orgId), orderBy('date', 'desc'))
if (filters.accountId)  q = query(q, where('accountId', '==', filters.accountId))
if (filters.categoryId) q = query(q, where('categoryId', '==', filters.categoryId))
if (filters.dateFrom)   q = query(q, where('date', '>=', Timestamp.fromDate(filters.dateFrom)))
if (filters.dateTo)     q = query(q, where('date', '<=', Timestamp.fromDate(filters.dateTo)))
```

It is used by `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/transactions/page.tsx:33` (filters from `components/transactions/TransactionFilters.tsx`: account, category, date from/to; amount and search are client-side) and, without filters, by the analytics pages (`cashflow`, `pl`, `calendar`).

| Combination reaching Firestore | Index |
|---|---|
| no filter / `date >=` and/or `date <=` + `orderBy(date desc)` | single-field `date` (automatic) |
| `accountId ==` (+ date range) + `orderBy(date desc)` | **composite `transactions`: `accountId ASC, date DESC`** |
| `categoryId ==` (+ date range) + `orderBy(date desc)` | **composite `transactions`: `categoryId ASC, date DESC`** |
| `accountId ==` + `categoryId ==` (+ date range) + `orderBy(date desc)` | **composite `transactions`: `accountId ASC, categoryId ASC, date DESC`** |
| `organizations` `where(members.<uid>.role != null)` (`lib/firebase/queries.ts`) | single-field on the map subfield (automatic; map subfields are indexed recursively) |
| unfiltered `onSnapshot` on `accounts`, `categories`, `bankConnections`, `getDocs(categories)` | none |

A range on `date` uses the same composite index as `orderBy('date', 'desc')`, because the inequality is on the ordered field. Index merging would cover two equalities plus `orderBy` without the three-field index. With a date range added, that is not guaranteed by the [index docs](https://firebase.google.com/docs/firestore/query-data/index-overview), so the third index is explicit. Every index uses `queryScope: COLLECTION`, because the queries are collection-scoped, not collection-group queries.

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `scripts/verify-firestore-rules.mjs` (full file)**

This is the production check for D6: rules and indexes, seen as a holder of the public web config. It runs unchanged against the emulators.

```js
// Black-box check of the deployed Firestore rules and composite indexes through
// the public REST API, i.e. what anyone holding the public web config can do.
//
// Prints only PASS/FAIL lines with HTTP statuses, never document contents.
// Creates one throwaway Auth user (always deleted at the end). Every
// organization document it manages to create is printed as LEFTOVER_ORG=<path>;
// clients cannot delete organizations by design, so remove each one with:
//   firebase firestore:delete "<path>" -r --force --project <projectId>
//
// Environment:
//   FIREBASE_API_KEY     required, public web API key
//   FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID  default "finflow-e94b5"
//   FIRESTORE_ORIGIN     default "https://firestore.googleapis.com"
//                        emulator: "http://127.0.0.1:8080"
//   AUTH_ORIGIN          default "https://identitytoolkit.googleapis.com"
//                        emulator: "http://127.0.0.1:9099/identitytoolkit.googleapis.com"

const apiKey = process.env.FIREBASE_API_KEY
if (!apiKey) {
  console.error('FIREBASE_API_KEY is not set')
  process.exit(2)
}
const projectId = process.env.FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID || 'finflow-e94b5'
const firestoreOrigin = process.env.FIRESTORE_ORIGIN || 'https://firestore.googleapis.com'
const authOrigin = process.env.AUTH_ORIGIN || 'https://identitytoolkit.googleapis.com'
const docs = `${firestoreOrigin}/v1/projects/${projectId}/databases/(default)/documents`
const key = `key=${encodeURIComponent(apiKey)}`

let failures = 0
const leftovers = []

function report(ok, label) {
  if (!ok) failures++
  console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${label}`)
}

function expectStatus(label, actual, expected) {
  report(actual === expected, `${label}: HTTP ${actual} (expected ${expected})`)
  return actual === expected
}

async function call(method, url, { token, body } = {}) {
  const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  const res = await fetch(url, { method, headers, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) })
  const text = await res.text()
  let json = null
  try {
    json = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null
  } catch {
    json = null
  }
  return { status: res.status, json }
}

// Same shape as lib/firebase/firestore.ts#createOrganization.
function orgDocument(ownerId, memberUids, plan = 'trial') {
  const now = Date.now()
  const role = { mapValue: { fields: { role: { stringValue: 'owner' } } } }
  return {
    fields: {
      name: { stringValue: 'rules-check (safe to delete)' },
      currency: { stringValue: 'UAH' },
      ownerId: { stringValue: ownerId },
      plan: { stringValue: plan },
      trialEndsAt: { timestampValue: new Date(now + 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString() },
      createdAt: { timestampValue: new Date(now).toISOString() },
      members: { mapValue: { fields: Object.fromEntries(memberUids.map((uid) => [uid, role])) } },
    },
  }
}

// POST a new organization; remember its path if the server accepted it.
async function createOrg(token, body) {
  const res = await call('POST', `${docs}/organizations?${key}`, { token, body })
  if (res.status === 200 && res.json?.name) leftovers.push(res.json.name.split('/documents/')[1])
  return res
}

// REST form of where(`members.${uid}.role`, '!=', null) from lib/firebase/queries.ts.
function memberQuery(uid) {
  return {
    structuredQuery: {
      from: [{ collectionId: 'organizations' }],
      where: { unaryFilter: { op: 'IS_NOT_NULL', field: { fieldPath: `members.\`${uid}\`.role` } } },
    },
  }
}

const unfilteredQuery = { structuredQuery: { from: [{ collectionId: 'organizations' }], limit: 1 } }

// REST form of lib/firebase/transactions.ts#getTransactions with filters.
function transactionsQuery(filters) {
  const fieldFilters = filters.map(([fieldPath, op, value]) => ({ fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath }, op, value } }))
  return {
    structuredQuery: {
      from: [{ collectionId: 'transactions' }],
      where: fieldFilters.length === 1 ? fieldFilters[0] : { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: fieldFilters } },
      orderBy: [{ field: { fieldPath: 'date' }, direction: 'DESCENDING' }],
      limit: 1,
    },
  }
}

// 1. No credentials at all.
expectStatus('anonymous list organizations', (await call('GET', `${docs}/organizations?pageSize=1&${key}`)).status, 403)
expectStatus('anonymous get _meta/health', (await call('GET', `${docs}/_meta/health?${key}`)).status, 403)

// 2. A freshly registered user who belongs to no organization.
const signUp = await call('POST', `${authOrigin}/v1/accounts:signUp?${key}`, {
  body: {
    email: `rules-check-${Date.now()}@example.com`,
    password: `Rc-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,
    returnSecureToken: true,
  },
})
if (signUp.status !== 200) {
  console.log(`FAIL sign up throwaway user: HTTP ${signUp.status} ${signUp.json?.error?.message ?? ''}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
const { idToken, localId: uid } = signUp.json

try {
  const rulesActive = expectStatus(
    'signed-in unfiltered list organizations',
    (await call('POST', `${docs}:runQuery?${key}`, { token: idToken, body: unfilteredQuery })).status,
    403,
  )
  if (!rulesActive) {
    console.log('FAIL the S00 rules are not active; aborting before any write')
  } else {
    expectStatus('create org with foreign ownerId', (await createOrg(idToken, orgDocument('someone-else', [uid]))).status, 403)
    expectStatus('create org with plan=pro', (await createOrg(idToken, orgDocument(uid, [uid], 'pro'))).status, 403)

    const before = leftovers.length
    const created = await createOrg(idToken, orgDocument(uid, [uid]))
    expectStatus('create own trial org', created.status, 200)

    if (created.status === 200) {
      const orgPath = leftovers[before]

      const listed = await call('POST', `${docs}:runQuery?${key}`, { token: idToken, body: memberQuery(uid) })
      expectStatus('member-filtered list organizations (useOrg query)', listed.status, 200)
      const count = Array.isArray(listed.json) ? listed.json.filter((row) => row.document).length : -1
      report(count === 1, `member-filtered list returns exactly the own org: ${count} document(s)`)

      expectStatus('member get own org', (await call('GET', `${docs}/${orgPath}?${key}`, { token: idToken })).status, 200)
      expectStatus(
        'member update name',
        (
          await call('PATCH', `${docs}/${orgPath}?updateMask.fieldPaths=name&${key}`, {
            token: idToken,
            body: { fields: { name: { stringValue: 'rules-check renamed (safe to delete)' } } },
          })
        ).status,
        200,
      )
      expectStatus(
        'member update plan',
        (
          await call('PATCH', `${docs}/${orgPath}?updateMask.fieldPaths=plan&${key}`, {
            token: idToken,
            body: { fields: { plan: { stringValue: 'pro' } } },
          })
        ).status,
        403,
      )
      expectStatus('member delete org', (await call('DELETE', `${docs}/${orgPath}?${key}`, { token: idToken })).status, 403)
      expectStatus(
        'member create account in own org',
        (
          await call('POST', `${docs}/${orgPath}/accounts?${key}`, {
            token: idToken,
            body: { fields: { name: { stringValue: 'rules-check' }, balance: { integerValue: '0' } } },
          })
        ).status,
        200,
      )

      // Composite indexes from firestore.indexes.json. A missing or still
      // building index answers HTTP 400 FAILED_PRECONDITION, even on an empty
      // collection. The emulator never requires indexes.
      const acc = ['accountId', 'EQUAL', { stringValue: 'rules-check-account' }]
      const cat = ['categoryId', 'EQUAL', { stringValue: 'rules-check-category' }]
      const from = ['date', 'GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL', { timestampValue: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z' }]
      for (const [label, filters] of [
        ['index accountId + date desc', [acc]],
        ['index categoryId + date desc', [cat]],
        ['index accountId + categoryId + date range desc', [acc, cat, from]],
      ]) {
        const res = await call('POST', `${docs}/${orgPath}:runQuery?${key}`, {
          token: idToken,
          body: transactionsQuery(filters),
        })
        expectStatus(label, res.status, 200)
      }
    }
  }
} finally {
  const deleted = await call('POST', `${authOrigin}/v1/accounts:delete?${key}`, { body: { idToken } })
  expectStatus('delete throwaway user', deleted.status, 200)
}

for (const path of leftovers) console.log(`LEFTOVER_ORG=${path}`)
console.log(failures === 0 ? 'ALL CHECKS PASSED' : `${failures} CHECK(S) FAILED`)
process.exit(failures === 0 ? 0 : 1)
```

Safety: the signed-in unfiltered list runs before any write. If it is not 403, the S00 rules are not live and the script writes nothing. Every successful org create is tracked and printed. During planning, a run against allow-all rules on the emulator printed `FAIL the S00 rules are not active; aborting before any write` and performed no create.

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

const sdk = vi.hoisted(() => ({

vi.mock('firebase/app', () => ({
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Wire the emulators in `lib/firebase/firebase.ts`**

Replace lines 1–4:

```ts

import { getAuth } from 'firebase/auth'
import { getFirestore } from 'firebase/firestore'
import { getStorage } from 'firebase/storage'
```

with:

```ts
import { initializeApp, getApps } from 'firebase/app'
import { getAuth, connectAuthEmulator } from 'firebase/auth'
import { getFirestore, connectFirestoreEmulator } from 'firebase/firestore'
import { getStorage } from 'firebase/storage'
```

Then replace lines 18–19:

```ts
export const storage = getStorage(app)
export default app
```

with:

```ts
export const storage = getStorage(app)

// Local dev and CI e2e: use the Auth + Firestore emulators (ports from firebase.json).
// The guard lives on globalThis so that re-evaluating this module (Next.js HMR)
// does not connect a second time; the SDK throws if an already-started
// Firestore/Auth instance is pointed at an emulator again.
const emulatorGuard = globalThis as typeof globalThis & {
  __finflowFirebaseEmulatorsConnected?: boolean
}
if (
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS === '1' &&
  !emulatorGuard.__finflowFirebaseEmulatorsConnected
) {
  emulatorGuard.__finflowFirebaseEmulatorsConnected = true
  connectAuthEmulator(auth, 'http://127.0.0.1:9099', { disableWarnings: true })
  connectFirestoreEmulator(db, '127.0.0.1', 8080)
}

export default app
```

The contract asks for a run-once guard. A plain module-level `let` would reset on HMR re-evaluation, while `getAuth`/`getFirestore` return the same, already-connected instances, and connecting them again throws. The guard therefore lives on `globalThis`. `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS` is inlined by Next at build time. It is set only in CI's e2e step and local emulator runs, never in Vercel (Part E).

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the failing client-SDK test `tests/rules/client-sdk.test.ts` (full file)**

It runs the real `lib/firebase/*` modules through the new wiring against the emulator as a signed-out browser. That proves the wiring works with the real SDK, and pins `getOrganization`'s contract: `null`, not a throw, for an org the user may not read or one that does not exist. Under the new `get` rule, a missing document is *denied*, not reported as "does not exist". `useOrg` calls `getOrganization(stored)` without a `catch`, so today a stale `lastOrgId_<uid>` would leave the user without an org.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { initializeTestEnvironment, type RulesTestEnvironment } from '@firebase/rules-unit-testing'
import { doc, setDoc, terminate, type Firestore } from 'firebase/firestore'

// Runs the real client modules (lib/firebase/*) against the Firestore emulator
// through the NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1 wiring, as a signed-out browser would.
let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment
let client: typeof import('@/lib/firebase/firestore')
let clientDb: Firestore

beforeAll(async () => {
  testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
    projectId: 'demo-finflow',
    firestore: { rules: readFileSync(resolve(process.cwd(), 'firestore.rules'), 'utf8') },
  })
  await testEnv.clearFirestore()
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (ctx) => {
    await setDoc(doc(ctx.firestore() as unknown as Firestore, 'organizations/orgB'), {
      name: 'B',
      currency: 'UAH',
      ownerId: 'bob',
      plan: 'trial',
      members: { bob: { role: 'owner' } },
    })
  })
  vi.stubEnv('NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS', '1')
  vi.stubEnv('NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID', 'demo-finflow')
  client = await import('@/lib/firebase/firestore')
  clientDb = (await import('@/lib/firebase/firebase')).db
})

afterAll(async () => {
  if (clientDb) await terminate(clientDb)
  vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  await testEnv?.cleanup()
})

describe('client SDK against the emulator (signed out)', () => {
  it('reaches the emulator: listing orgs is rejected by the rules', async () => {
    await expect(client.getUserOrganizations('bob')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'permission-denied' })
  })

  it('getOrganization returns null for an org the caller may not read', async () => {
    await expect(client.getOrganization('orgB')).resolves.toBeNull()
  })

  it('getOrganization returns null for an org id that does not exist', async () => {
    await expect(client.getOrganization('missing-org')).resolves.toBeNull()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Map `permission-denied` to `null` in `getOrganization`**

In `lib/firebase/firestore.ts`, replace (lines 37–40 after D3):

```ts
export async function getOrganization(orgId: string): Promise<Organization | null> {
  const orgDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'organizations', orgId))

  if (!orgDoc.exists()) return null
```

with:

```ts
// firestore.rules deny `get` both for orgs the user is not a member of and for
// org ids that do not exist (resource == null), so permission-denied means
// "no such org for this user", exactly like a missing document.
// The code is checked structurally: `instanceof FirestoreError` is not reliable
// when the SDK is loaded more than once (e.g. under Vitest).
function isPermissionDenied(e: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof e === 'object' && e !== null && (e as { code?: unknown }).code === 'permission-denied'
}

async function getOrganizationSnapshot(orgId: string) {
  try {
    return await getDoc(doc(db, 'organizations', orgId))
  } catch (e) {
    if (isPermissionDenied(e)) return null
    throw e
  }
}

export async function getOrganization(orgId: string): Promise<Organization | null> {
  const orgDoc = await getOrganizationSnapshot(orgId)

  if (!orgDoc || !orgDoc.exists()) return null
```

The rest of `getOrganization` (mapping `data` to `Organization`) stays unchanged.

## Part E: Playwright smoke on emulators, CI, Vercel project + deploy, EB redirect, branch protection, Cloudflare decommission

This part adds Playwright on `next dev` against the Auth and Firestore emulators (a smoke flow plus API-guard checks), a GitHub Actions `ci` job, and the Vercel project `finflow` (region `fra1`, Node 24.x, env vars uploaded from local gitignored files). It then ships S00 through a PR to `master`, checks production, adds the Enable Banking redirect URL, protects `master` and shuts down Cloudflare Pages. **Preconditions:** Part A Tasks A1–A3, Parts B and C, and Part D Tasks D1–D5 are committed on `s00-platform`: A (Next 16 without Cloudflare, the contract npm scripts), B (`firebase-admin`, `/api/health`), C (Enable Banking hardening, backdoor removed) and D (`firebase.json`, Firestore/Storage rules and their tests, emulator wiring in `lib/firebase/firebase.ts`). Part A Task A4 (docs) runs between E4 and E5, so it is the last commit in the S00 PR. Part D's production rules deploy (Task D6) runs right after Task E5 is green on production, and Task E8 starts only after D6.

### Conventions for this part

- Shell: **Git Bash**. Every command block starts with `cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1` (repo root), and blocks that write, delete, install, commit or push also run the repo check from **How to read this plan**. The cwd resets between tool calls, and the session's default cwd is a different repo. Create and edit files with absolute paths (`F:/AI/finflow/...`).
- **Never print a secret.** The commands below read secrets from `.env`, `.env.local` and `.secrets/` and print only key names, lengths or PASS/FAIL. Do not `cat` those files.
- **No leading slash in API paths** passed to native programs (`gh api repos/...`, `node scripts/vercel.mjs ...`). Git Bash rewrites `/v9/...` into `C:/Program Files/Git/v9/...`.
- Vercel is driven only through its **REST API**, called with Node 24 `fetch` from `scripts/vercel.mjs` (Task E4). curl is not used for three reasons: there is no `jq` on this host, JSON-escaping a multi-line PEM in shell is fragile, and this workstation's context-mode hook intercepts `curl`/`wget` and inline `node -e "fetch(...)"` one-liners. Node scripts from a file or a `node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'` heredoc are not intercepted.
- Inline `node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'` scripts contain **no double backslashes** on purpose, because some shell bridges collapse `\\` to `\`. Keep it that way if you edit them.
- Steps marked **[USER]** or **[USER-CONFIRM]** need the human. Send the quoted message (Ukrainian), wait for the answer, and do not continue past that step without it.
- Long waits: `gh run watch` and `node scripts/vercel.mjs wait` can run longer than a 10-minute tool limit. If the tool times out, run the same command again (both resume), or run it in the background.

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace `playwright.config.ts`**

Notes:
- `webServer.env` is merged over `process.env`; Playwright's `WebServerPlugin` spawns with `{ ...DEFAULT_ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLES, ...process.env, ...options.env }`.
- Next.js never overrides a variable that is already in `process.env` with a value from `.env.local`. So these demo values win locally too, and a local run cannot reach production Firebase.
- `reuseExistingServer: false` everywhere, not only in CI. A dev server started by hand reads `.env.local` (production Firebase), and the smoke test would create real users there.
- `EB_STATE_SECRET` is a dummy. Without it the callback answers `500 server not configured` instead of `400 invalid state` (Part C Task C4).

Full file content:

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test'

const isCI = !!process.env.CI

// Env for the `next dev` server under test. Dummy, public-shaped values for the
// emulator-only demo project "demo-finflow" — none of these are secrets.
// Explicit values here win over .env.local (Next.js never overrides variables that
// are already set in process.env), so a local run can never reach production Firebase.
const E2E_SERVER_ENV: Record<string, string> = {
  NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS: '1',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY: 'demo-api-key',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN: 'demo-finflow.firebaseapp.com',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID: 'demo-finflow',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET: 'demo-finflow.appspot.com',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID: '000000000000',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID: '1:000000000000:web:0000000000000000',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MEASUREMENT_ID: 'G-DEMO000000',
  NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL: 'http://localhost:3000',
  EB_STATE_SECRET: 'e2e-only-state-secret',
  NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: '1',
}

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './tests/e2e',
  timeout: 120_000,
  expect: { timeout: 20_000 },
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  retries: isCI ? 1 : 0,
  forbidOnly: isCI,
  reporter: isCI ? [['list'], ['html', { open: 'never' }]] : 'list',
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:3000',
    headless: true,
    // The app buckets transactions by the browser's local month; UTC keeps the
    // smoke assertion deterministic on every machine.
    timezoneId: 'UTC',
    navigationTimeout: 90_000,
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
    screenshot: 'only-on-failure',
    video: 'off',
  },
  projects: [{ name: 'chromium', use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] } }],
  webServer: {
    command: 'npm run dev',
    url: 'http://localhost:3000',
    env: E2E_SERVER_ENV,
    // Never reuse a server: one started by hand may point at production Firebase.
    reuseExistingServer: false,
    timeout: 180_000,
    stdout: 'pipe',
    stderr: 'pipe',
  },
})
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the API-guard spec (acceptance criterion 4, no emulators needed)**

Create `tests/e2e/api-guards.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test'

// S00 spec, readiness criterion 4. No emulators needed: every request must be rejected
// before the route touches Firestore or Enable Banking.
test.describe('API guards', () => {
  test('test backdoors are gone (404)', async ({ request }) => {
    expect((await request.get('/api/test/auth?email=a%40b.c&password=x')).status()).toBe(404)
    expect((await request.get('/test-login')).status()).toBe(404)
  })

  test('Enable Banking routes require a bearer token (401)', async ({ request }) => {
    const session = await request.post('/api/enablebanking/session', {
      data: { aspspName: 'Bank Millennium', aspspCountry: 'PL', orgId: 'victim-org' },
    })
    expect(session.status()).toBe(401)
    expect((await request.get('/api/enablebanking/institutions?country=PL')).status()).toBe(401)
    expect((await request.get('/api/enablebanking/accounts?sessionId=s&orgId=victim-org')).status()).toBe(401)
  })

  test('Enable Banking callback rejects a forged state before exchanging the code (400)', async ({ request }) => {
    // The pre-S00 format: unsigned base64url JSON with just an orgId.
    const forged = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ orgId: 'victim-org' })).toString('base64url')
    const res = await request.get(`/api/enablebanking/callback?code=fake-code&state=${forged}`, { maxRedirects: 0 })
    expect(res.status()).toBe(400)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'invalid state' })
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the smoke spec**

Create `tests/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { test, expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test'

// Month labels exactly as lib/utils/analytics.ts renders them: UA_MONTHS + 2-digit year.
const UA_MONTHS = ['Січ', 'Лют', 'Бер', 'Кві', 'Тра', 'Чер', 'Лип', 'Сер', 'Вер', 'Жов', 'Лис', 'Гру']

const NEEDS_EMULATORS =
  'Smoke needs the Auth + Firestore emulators. Run: firebase emulators:exec --only auth,firestore --project demo-finflow "npm run test:e2e"'

// React attaches __reactProps$<id> to DOM nodes when it hydrates them. Filling a
// controlled input before that loses the value (the old tests worked around this with
// the /test-login backdoor), so wait for it explicitly.
async function waitForHydration(page: Page, selector: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.waitForFunction((sel) => {
    const el = document.querySelector(sel)
    return !!el && Object.keys(el).some((k) => k.startsWith('__reactProps$'))
  }, selector)
}

test.describe('smoke on emulators', () => {
  test.skip(!process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST || !process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST, NEEDS_EMULATORS)

  test('GET /api/health reads Firestore through firebase-admin', async ({ request }) => {
    const res = await request.get('/api/health')
    expect(res.status()).toBe(200)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ ok: true, region: 'local', firestore: 'ok' })
  })

  test('Enable Banking session from a signed-in non-member -> 403 (criterion 4)', async ({ request }) => {
    // A real Auth-emulator user who belongs to no organization. The route runs
    // requireOrgMember before createJwt, so it answers 403 without calling Enable Banking.
    const signUp = await request.post(
      `http://${process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST}/identitytoolkit.googleapis.com/v1/accounts:signUp?key=demo-api-key`,
      { data: { email: `nonmember-${Date.now()}@finflow.test`, password: 'nonmember-pass-123', returnSecureToken: true } },
    )
    expect(signUp.status()).toBe(200)
    const { idToken } = (await signUp.json()) as { idToken: string }
    const res = await request.post('/api/enablebanking/session', {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${idToken}` },
      data: { aspspName: 'Bank Millennium', aspspCountry: 'PL', orgId: 'victim-org' },
    })
    expect(res.status()).toBe(403)
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'forbidden' })
  })

  test('register -> company -> account -> income -> Cash Flow shows it', async ({ page }) => {
    const runId = `${Date.now()}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`
    const email = `smoke-${runId}@finflow.test`
    const orgName = `Smoke Co ${runId}`
    const accountName = `Smoke Bank ${runId}`
    const amount = '777' // < 1000, so uk-UA formatting adds no group separator

    // 1. Register: one submit creates the Auth user and organizations/{orgId}.
    await page.goto('/register')
    await waitForHydration(page, '#name')
    await page.getByLabel('Your name', { exact: true }).fill('Smoke Tester')
    await page.getByLabel('Email', { exact: true }).fill(email)
    await page.getByLabel('Password', { exact: true }).fill('smoke-pass-123')
    await page.getByLabel('Company name', { exact: true }).fill(orgName)
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Try 14 days free' }).click()

    // 2. Redirect to /{orgId}/transactions. The sidebar shows the org name only after
    //    useOrg loaded it, which exercises the organizations get/list security rules.
    await page.waitForURL(/\/[A-Za-z0-9]{20}\/transactions$/)
    const orgId = new URL(page.url()).pathname.split('/')[1]
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Транзакції' })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.locator('aside').getByText(orgName, { exact: true })).toBeVisible()

    // 3. Add an account (components/forms/AccountForm.tsx).
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: '+ Додати рахунок', exact: true }).click()
    const accountDialog = page.getByRole('dialog')
    await expect(accountDialog.getByText('Новий рахунок', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await accountDialog.getByLabel('Назва', { exact: true }).fill(accountName)
    await accountDialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Створити', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(accountDialog).toBeHidden()
    // Scoped to <p>: the account filter <select> also gets an <option> with this name.
    await expect(page.locator('p', { hasText: accountName })).toBeVisible()

    // 4. Add an income transaction on that account (components/forms/TransactionForm.tsx).
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Дохід', exact: true }).click()
    const txDialog = page.getByRole('dialog')
    await expect(txDialog.getByText('Нова транзакція', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    const incomePanel = txDialog.getByRole('tabpanel')
    // The "Рахунок" <select> has no label association; it is the first native select in the panel.
    await incomePanel.locator('select').first().selectOption({ label: accountName })
    await incomePanel.getByLabel('Сума', { exact: true }).fill(amount)
    await txDialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Додати', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(txDialog).toBeHidden()

    // 5. Cash Flow (sidebar "Analytics" -> /{orgId}/analytics/cashflow) shows the income
    //    in the current month row and in the "Доходи" summary card.
    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Analytics' }).click()
    await page.waitForURL(`**/${orgId}/analytics/cashflow`)
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Cash Flow' })).toBeVisible()
    const now = new Date()
    const monthLabel = `${UA_MONTHS[now.getUTCMonth()]} ${String(now.getUTCFullYear()).slice(2)}`
    const monthRow = page.getByRole('row').filter({ hasText: monthLabel })
    await expect(monthRow.getByRole('cell').nth(1)).toHaveText(`+${amount}`)
    await expect(page.getByText('Доходи', { exact: true }).locator('..')).toContainText(amount)
  })
})
```

- Waiting at the sidebar org name → the `organizations` list/get rule rejects `useOrg`'s query (Part D).

- `/api/health` 500 → the `getAdmin()` emulator branch (Part B). If the server log shows `Could not load the default credentials`, Firestore was initialised with `preferRest: true` on the emulator; Task B3 must pass `preferRest: !process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST`.
- The non-member test gets 401 instead of 403 → the dev server cannot reach the Auth emulator (`FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST` missing in its env); 500 → the Firestore read in `requireOrgMember` failed (same `preferRest` cause as above).

Action versions are the latest majors as of 2026-10-22 (checked via `gh api repos/<action>/releases/latest`): checkout v7.0.1, setup-node v7.1.0, setup-java v6.1.0, cache v6.1.0, upload-artifact v7.0.2. - [ ] **Step 2: Create `.github/workflows/ci.yml`**

```sh

[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
```
