Full file content:

```yaml
name: CI

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches: [master]

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

jobs:
  # The job id "ci" is the required status check on master (Task E7). Do not rename it.
  ci:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    env:
      NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: '1'
      # Dummy, public-shaped config for the emulator-only demo project. These are not secrets.
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY: demo-api-key
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN: demo-finflow.firebaseapp.com
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID: demo-finflow
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET: demo-finflow.appspot.com
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID: '000000000000'
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID: '1:000000000000:web:0000000000000000'
      NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MEASUREMENT_ID: G-DEMO000000
      NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL: http://localhost:3000
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Set up Node.js 24
        uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: 24
          cache: npm

      - name: Set up Java 21 (Firestore emulator)
        uses: actions/setup-java@v6
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Cache Firebase emulator binaries
        uses: actions/cache@v6
        with:
          path: ~/.cache/firebase/emulators
          key: firebase-emulators-${{ runner.os }}-firebase-tools-15

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Install Firebase CLI
        run: npm i -g firebase-tools@15

      - name: Lint
        run: npm run lint

      - name: Typecheck
        run: npm run typecheck

      - name: Unit tests
        run: npm run test:unit

      - name: Firestore rules tests (emulator)
        run: npm run test:rules

      - name: Install Playwright Chromium
        run: npx playwright install --with-deps chromium

      - name: E2E smoke (next dev + Auth/Firestore emulators)
        env:
          NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS: '1'
          EB_STATE_SECRET: ci-e2e-only-state-secret
        run: firebase emulators:exec --only auth,firestore --project demo-finflow "npm run test:e2e"

      - name: Build
        run: npm run build

      - name: Upload Playwright report and emulator logs
        if: failure()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: |
            playwright-report/
            test-results/
            firebase-debug.log
            firestore-debug.log
          retention-days: 7
          if-no-files-found: ignore
```

Why the job-level `NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_*`: `lib/firebase/firebase.ts` calls `getFirestore()` at import time, and the root layout imports it. Without a `projectId`, `next build` fails while prerendering. `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS` is set **only** on the e2e step, so the build step compiles the production code path.

On failure, use the `gh run view <RUN_ID> --log-failed` and report-download commands from Task E5 Step 5, fix the cause in the owning task's files (Part D for rules, Part B for `getAdmin`, and so on), commit with the `Co-Authored-By` trailer, `git push`, and watch the new run. ### Task 23: Vercel project `finflow`, region `fra1`, environment variables (E4)

**Files:**
- Create: `vercel.json`
- Create: `scripts/vercel.mjs` (Vercel REST helper; ESLint-clean under the repo config, not part of `tsc` because `tsconfig.json` does not include `*.mjs`)
- Modify (only if needed): `package.json` `"engines"`, see Step 3
- Local only, never committed: `.env.local` (Step 1 appends missing public Firebase keys), `.secrets/enablebanking-app-id.txt` (Step 7, from the user)

REST endpoints used (from https://vercel.com/docs/rest-api, checked on 2026-10-22). Every call adds `?teamId=$VERCEL_TEAM_ID`.
- `POST v11/projects`: `name`, `framework`, `gitRepository {type:'github', repo:'owner/name'}`, `serverlessFunctionRegion`
- `PATCH v9/projects/{idOrName}`: `nodeVersion` (enum includes `24.x`)
- `GET v9/projects/{idOrName}` and `GET v9/projects/{idOrName}/domains?production=true`
- `POST v10/projects/{idOrName}/env?upsert=true`: array body `{key, value, type: 'plain'|'sensitive'|'encrypted'|'system', target[]}`, response `{created, failed[]}`
- `GET v10/projects/{idOrName}/env`
- `GET v7/deployments` with `projectId` / `target` / `branch` / `sha` / `limit`; `readyState` can be `BLOCKED` with `seatBlock.blockCode` (for example `COMMIT_AUTHOR_REQUIRED`); `source` tells how the deployment was created (`git` for the Git integration)
- `POST v13/deployments`: `gitSource {type:'github', org, repo, ref}`, `target:'production'`

Env-var plan:
- The 7 `NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_*` are `plain` for production, preview and development.
- `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` is `plain` for production and preview, set to the production domain.
- `ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID`, `ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY` (PEM with real newlines), `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` (base64 of the SA JSON file) and `EB_STATE_SECRET` (fresh 32 random bytes, base64, never the local one) are `sensitive` for production and preview only. Sensitive values are write-only, so a Development copy would be useless for `vercel env pull`.
- **Never** set `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS` in Vercel.

State verified read-only on 2026-10-22:
- Team `biuro-3838s-projects` has **no** `finflow` project yet.
- The Vercel GitHub integration is already installed on the `remit-agency` namespace (`ownerType: user`, `isAccessRestricted: false`).
- `remit-agency/finflow` is a **public** repo owned by a GitHub **user**.

- [ ] **Step 1: Make sure `.env.local` has the 7 public Firebase values (from `wrangler.toml` history if needed)**

Part A normally copied them from `wrangler.toml` `[vars]` before deleting it. This step re-derives any that are missing from commit `b68ecca`, which still contains `wrangler.toml`. It prints key names only.

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'
import fs from 'node:fs'
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process'
const KEYS = [
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MEASUREMENT_ID',
]
const file = '.env.local'
const current = fs.existsSync(file) ? fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8') : ''
const present = new Set(current.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.match(/^([A-Z0-9_]+)=./)?.[1]).filter(Boolean))
const missing = KEYS.filter((k) => !present.has(k))
if (missing.length === 0) {
  console.log('.env.local already has all 7 public Firebase keys')
  process.exit(0)
}
const toml = execFileSync('git', ['show', 'b68ecca:wrangler.toml'], { encoding: 'utf8' })
const lines = missing.map((k) => {
  const m = toml.match(new RegExp('^' + k + String.raw`\s*=\s*"([^"]*)"`, 'm'))
  if (!m) throw new Error(`${k} not found in wrangler.toml at b68ecca`)
  return `${k}=${m[1]}`
})
fs.appendFileSync(file, `${current && !current.endsWith('\n') ? '\n' : ''}${lines.join('\n')}\n`)
console.log(`added to .env.local: ${missing.join(', ')}`)
EOF
cut -d= -f1 .env.local | sort
git check-ignore -v .env.local
```

Expected: `.env.local already has all 7 public Firebase keys` or `added to .env.local: ...`. The key list contains all 7 `NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_*` names, and `git check-ignore` prints a `.env*` rule.

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `scripts/vercel.mjs`**

Full file content:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Vercel REST API helper for the finflow project (S00, Part E).
//
// - Reads VERCEL_TOKEN and VERCEL_TEAM_ID from .env (or from the process environment).
// - Never prints secret values: they go straight from local gitignored files to the API.
// - API paths are written without a leading slash because Git Bash rewrites "/v9/..."
//   arguments into Windows paths when it starts native programs such as node.exe.
//
// Usage (run from the repo root):
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs project-create   create or reuse project "finflow" (GitHub remit-agency/finflow, Node 24.x)
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs project-show     print framework, Node version, Git link and region
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs domains [--quiet]
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs env-sync [--dry-run] [--only=K1,K2] [--skip=K1,K2] [--app-url=https://host] [--rotate-state-secret]
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs env-list         plain values are shown, secret values are not
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs deployments [--target=production|preview] [--branch=name] [--sha=fullsha]
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs wait [--target=...] [--branch=...] [--sha=...] [--timeout=seconds]
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs redeploy-prod    new production deployment of master, created by the token owner
//   node scripts/vercel.mjs smoke-prod [--url=https://host]
import fs from 'node:fs'
import crypto from 'node:crypto'

const PROJECT = 'finflow'
const GIT_ORG = 'remit-agency'
const GIT_REPO = 'finflow'
const REGION = 'fra1'
const NODE_VERSION = '24.x'
const FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID = 'finflow-e94b5'
const ALL_TARGETS = ['production', 'preview', 'development']
const SECRET_TARGETS = ['production', 'preview']
const PUBLIC_FIREBASE_KEYS = [
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID',
  'NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MEASUREMENT_ID',
]
const EB_APP_ID_FILE = '.secrets/enablebanking-app-id.txt'
const EB_KEY_FILE = '.secrets/enablebanking-private.key'
const EB_CERT_FILE = '.secrets/enablebanking-public.crt'
const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i

// Failures throw instead of calling process.exit(): on Windows, exiting while fetch() sockets
// are still open aborts Node with "Assertion failed: !(handle->flags & UV_HANDLE_CLOSING)".
class Fatal extends Error {}

function die(message) {
  throw new Fatal(message)
}

function readDotenv(file) {
  const out = {}
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out
  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const m = line.match(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*?)\s*$/)
    if (!m) continue
    let value = m[2]
    if (value.length >= 2 && (value[0] === '"' || value[0] === "'") && value.at(-1) === value[0]) {
      value = value.slice(1, -1)
    }
    out[m[1]] = value
  }
  return out
}

function parseFlags(argv) {
  const flags = {}
  for (const arg of argv) {
    const m = arg.match(/^--([a-z0-9-]+)(?:=(.*))?$/)
    if (!m) die(`unknown argument: ${arg}`)
    flags[m[1]] = m[2] ?? true
  }
  return flags
}

const dotenv = readDotenv('.env')
const TOKEN = process.env.VERCEL_TOKEN || dotenv.VERCEL_TOKEN
const TEAM = process.env.VERCEL_TEAM_ID || dotenv.VERCEL_TEAM_ID

async function api(method, apiPath, body) {
  if (!TOKEN || !TEAM) die('VERCEL_TOKEN / VERCEL_TEAM_ID are missing in .env')
  const p = apiPath.replace(/^\/+/, '')
  const url = `https://api.vercel.com/${p}${p.includes('?') ? '&' : '?'}teamId=${encodeURIComponent(TEAM)}`
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${TOKEN}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  })
  const text = await res.text()
  let json = {}
  try {
    json = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {}
  } catch {
    json = { raw: text.slice(0, 300) }
  }
  return { status: res.status, ok: res.ok, json }
}

function must(result, what) {
  if (!result.ok) {
    const e = result.json.error ?? {}
    die(`${what}: HTTP ${result.status} ${e.code ?? ''} ${e.message ?? JSON.stringify(result.json).slice(0, 300)}`)
  }
  return result.json
}

// ---------- project ----------

async function projectCreate() {
  const existing = await api('GET', `v9/projects/${PROJECT}`)
  if (existing.ok) {
    console.log(`project exists: ${existing.json.id}`)
  } else if (existing.status === 404) {
    const created = must(
      await api('POST', 'v11/projects', {
        name: PROJECT,
        framework: 'nextjs',
        gitRepository: { type: 'github', repo: `${GIT_ORG}/${GIT_REPO}` },
        serverlessFunctionRegion: REGION,
      }),
      'POST v11/projects',
    )
    console.log(`project created: ${created.id}`)
  } else {
    must(existing, `GET v9/projects/${PROJECT}`)
  }
  must(await api('PATCH', `v9/projects/${PROJECT}`, { nodeVersion: NODE_VERSION }), 'PATCH nodeVersion')
  await projectShow()
}

async function projectShow() {
  const p = must(await api('GET', `v9/projects/${PROJECT}`), `GET v9/projects/${PROJECT}`)
  const summary = {
    id: p.id,
    name: p.name,
    framework: p.framework,
    nodeVersion: p.nodeVersion,
    gitRepo: p.link ? `${p.link.org}/${p.link.repo}` : null,
    productionBranch: p.link?.productionBranch ?? null,
    serverlessFunctionRegion: p.serverlessFunctionRegion ?? null,
  }
  console.log(JSON.stringify(summary, null, 2))
}

// ---------- domains ----------

async function prodDomains() {
  const r = must(await api('GET', `v9/projects/${PROJECT}/domains?production=true`), 'GET project domains')
  return (r.domains ?? []).filter((d) => !d.redirect)
}

function pickVercelDomain(list) {
  const names = list
    .filter((d) => d.name.endsWith('.vercel.app') && !d.gitBranch)
    .map((d) => d.name)
    .sort((a, b) => a.length - b.length)
  return names[0] ?? null
}

async function domains(flags) {
  const list = await prodDomains()
  const chosen = pickVercelDomain(list)
  if (flags.quiet) {
    if (chosen) console.log(chosen)
    return
  }
  for (const d of list) console.log(`${d.name}\tverified=${d.verified}\tgitBranch=${d.gitBranch ?? '-'}`)
  console.log(`PROD_DOMAIN=${chosen ?? ''}`)
}

// ---------- env ----------

function ebAppId() {
  const fromLocal = readDotenv('.env.local').ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID
  const fromFile = fs.existsSync(EB_APP_ID_FILE) ? fs.readFileSync(EB_APP_ID_FILE, 'utf8') : ''
  const value = (fromLocal || fromFile).trim()
  if (!UUID_RE.test(value)) {
    die(`ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID is missing or not a UUID (looked in .env.local and ${EB_APP_ID_FILE})`)
  }
  return value
}

function ebPrivateKey() {
  const pem = fs.readFileSync(EB_KEY_FILE, 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').trim() + '\n'
  if (!pem.startsWith('-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----')) die(`${EB_KEY_FILE} must be a PKCS#8 PEM (BEGIN PRIVATE KEY)`)
  if (fs.existsSync(EB_CERT_FILE)) {
    const fromKey = crypto.createPublicKey(pem).export({ type: 'spki', format: 'der' })
    const fromCert = new crypto.X509Certificate(fs.readFileSync(EB_CERT_FILE)).publicKey.export({
      type: 'spki',
      format: 'der',
    })
    if (!fromKey.equals(fromCert)) die(`${EB_KEY_FILE} does not match ${EB_CERT_FILE}`)
  }
  return pem
}

function serviceAccountBase64() {
  const files = fs.readdirSync('.secrets').filter((f) => /^finflow-e94b5-firebase-adminsdk-.+\.json$/.test(f))
  if (files.length !== 1) die(`expected exactly one service-account JSON in .secrets/, found ${files.length}`)
  const raw = fs.readFileSync(`.secrets/${files[0]}`, 'utf8')
  let sa
  try {
    sa = JSON.parse(raw)
  } catch {
    // Do not rethrow: Node's JSON error message quotes part of the (secret) input.
    die(`.secrets/${files[0]} is not valid JSON`)
  }
  if (sa.type !== 'service_account' || sa.project_id !== FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID || !sa.client_email) {
    die(`.secrets/${files[0]} is not a ${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID} service account`)
  }
  if (!String(sa.private_key ?? '').includes('BEGIN PRIVATE KEY')) die(`.secrets/${files[0]} has no private_key`)
  return Buffer.from(raw, 'utf8').toString('base64')
}

async function listEnv() {
  const r = must(await api('GET', `v10/projects/${PROJECT}/env`), 'GET env')
  return r.envs ?? []
}

async function envSync(flags) {
  const dryRun = Boolean(flags['dry-run'])
  const keySet = (value) => (typeof value === 'string' ? new Set(value.split(',').map((k) => k.trim())) : null)
  const only = keySet(flags.only)
  const skip = keySet(flags.skip) ?? new Set()
  const wanted = (key) => (!only || only.has(key)) && !skip.has(key)
  const local = readDotenv('.env.local')
  const entries = []

  for (const key of PUBLIC_FIREBASE_KEYS) {
    if (!wanted(key)) continue
    if (!local[key]) die(`${key} is missing in .env.local (Task E4 Step 1)`)
    entries.push({ key, value: local[key], type: 'plain', target: ALL_TARGETS })
  }
  if (wanted('NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID') && local.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID !== FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID) {
    die(`.env.local NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID must be ${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}, not the emulator demo project`)
  }

  if (wanted('NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL')) {
    let appUrl = typeof flags['app-url'] === 'string' ? flags['app-url'] : null
    if (!appUrl && !dryRun) {
      const chosen = pickVercelDomain(await prodDomains())
      if (chosen) appUrl = `https://${chosen}`
    }
    if (appUrl) {
      const u = new URL(appUrl)
      if (u.protocol !== 'https:' || u.pathname !== '/' || u.search) die('--app-url must look like https://host')
      entries.push({ key: 'NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL', value: u.origin, type: 'plain', target: SECRET_TARGETS })
      console.log(`NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL will be ${u.origin}`)
    } else {
      console.log('NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL: skipped (no production domain yet; Task E6 sets it)')
    }
  }

  if (wanted('ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID')) {
    entries.push({ key: 'ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID', value: ebAppId(), type: 'sensitive', target: SECRET_TARGETS })
  }
  if (wanted('ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY')) {
    entries.push({ key: 'ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY', value: ebPrivateKey(), type: 'sensitive', target: SECRET_TARGETS })
  }
  if (wanted('FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT')) {
    entries.push({ key: 'FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT', value: serviceAccountBase64(), type: 'sensitive', target: SECRET_TARGETS })
  }
  if (wanted('EB_STATE_SECRET')) {
    const exists = dryRun ? false : (await listEnv()).some((e) => e.key === 'EB_STATE_SECRET')
    if (exists && !flags['rotate-state-secret']) {
      console.log('EB_STATE_SECRET: kept the existing value (pass --rotate-state-secret to replace it)')
    } else {
      entries.push({
        key: 'EB_STATE_SECRET',
        value: crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('base64'),
        type: 'sensitive',
        target: SECRET_TARGETS,
      })
    }
  }

  for (const e of entries) {
    console.log(`${dryRun ? 'would set' : 'set'}\t${e.key}\t${e.type}\t${e.target.join(',')}\tlen=${e.value.length}`)
  }
  if (dryRun || entries.length === 0) return

  const r = must(await api('POST', `v10/projects/${PROJECT}/env?upsert=true`, entries), 'POST env')
  const failed = r.failed ?? []
  for (const f of failed) {
    console.error(`FAILED ${f.error?.envVarKey ?? f.error?.key ?? '?'}: ${f.error?.code ?? ''} ${f.error?.message ?? ''}`)
  }
  if (failed.length > 0) die(`${failed.length} variable(s) failed`)
  console.log(`ok: ${entries.length} variable(s) upserted`)
}

async function envList() {
  for (const e of await listEnv()) {
    const shown = e.type === 'plain' ? (e.value ?? '(n/a)') : '(hidden)'
    console.log(`${e.key}\t${e.type}\t${(e.target ?? []).join(',')}\t${shown}`)
  }
}

// ---------- deployments ----------

function deploymentQuery(flags, limit) {
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ projectId: PROJECT, limit: String(limit) })
  for (const k of ['target', 'branch', 'sha']) {
    if (typeof flags[k] === 'string') q.set(k, flags[k])
  }
  return `v7/deployments?${q}`
}

// Last column: how the deployment was created. A push through the Git integration shows
// `git`; criterion 1 ("push to master deploys") needs such a deployment, not one from
// `redeploy-prod` (created through the API by the token owner).
function describeDeployment(d) {
  return [
    d.uid,
    d.target ?? 'preview',
    d.readyState ?? d.state,
    d.meta?.githubCommitRef ?? '-',
    (d.meta?.githubCommitSha ?? '').slice(0, 7) || '-',
    d.url ? `https://${d.url}` : '-',
    d.source ?? '-',
  ].join('\t')
}

async function deployments(flags) {
  const r = must(await api('GET', deploymentQuery(flags, 5)), 'GET deployments')
  for (const d of r.deployments ?? []) console.log(describeDeployment(d))
}

async function wait(flags) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + Number(flags.timeout ?? 900) * 1000
  let last = ''
  for (;;) {
    const r = must(await api('GET', deploymentQuery(flags, 1)), 'GET deployments')
    const d = (r.deployments ?? [])[0]
    const state = d ? (d.readyState ?? d.state) : 'NOT_FOUND'
    const line = d ? describeDeployment(d) : 'no matching deployment yet'
    if (line !== last) {
      console.log(`${new Date().toISOString()}  ${line}`)
      last = line
    }
    if (state === 'READY') return
    if (['ERROR', 'CANCELED', 'BLOCKED'].includes(state)) {
      if (d?.seatBlock) console.error(`seatBlock: ${JSON.stringify(d.seatBlock)}`)
      if (d?.errorMessage) console.error(`errorMessage: ${d.errorMessage}`)
      die(`deployment ended in state ${state}`)
    }
    if (Date.now() > deadline) die('timed out waiting for the deployment')
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 15000))
  }
}

async function redeployProd() {
  const d = must(
    await api('POST', 'v13/deployments', {
      name: PROJECT,
      project: PROJECT,
      target: 'production',
      gitSource: { type: 'github', org: GIT_ORG, repo: GIT_REPO, ref: 'master' },
    }),
    'POST v13/deployments',
  )
  console.log(`created ${d.id}\t${d.readyState ?? ''}\thttps://${d.url}`)
}

// ---------- production acceptance checks (S00 spec, criteria 2 and 4) ----------

async function smokeProd(flags) {
  let base = typeof flags.url === 'string' ? flags.url : null
  if (!base) {
    const chosen = pickVercelDomain(await prodDomains())
    if (!chosen) die('no production domain yet; pass --url=https://host')
    base = `https://${chosen}`
  }
  base = new URL(base).origin
  console.log(`checking ${base}`)
  const results = []
  const check = async (name, fn) => {
    try {
      const detail = await fn()
      results.push(true)
      console.log(`PASS  ${name}${detail ? `  ${detail}` : ''}`)
    } catch (e) {
      results.push(false)
      console.log(`FAIL  ${name}  ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`)
    }
  }
  const call = (p, init = {}) => fetch(`${base}${p}`, { redirect: 'manual', ...init })
  const expectStatus = (res, status) => {
    if (res.status !== status) throw new Error(`expected HTTP ${status}, got ${res.status}`)
  }
  const forgedState = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ orgId: 'victim-org' })).toString('base64url')

  await check('GET /api/health -> ok, fra1, firestore ok', async () => {
    const res = await call('/api/health')
    expectStatus(res, 200)
    const body = await res.json()
    if (body.ok !== true || body.region !== REGION || body.firestore !== 'ok') {
      throw new Error(`unexpected body ${JSON.stringify(body)}`)
    }
    return JSON.stringify(body)
  })
  await check('GET /api/test/auth -> 404', async () => expectStatus(await call('/api/test/auth'), 404))
  await check('GET /test-login -> 404', async () => expectStatus(await call('/test-login'), 404))
  await check('POST /api/enablebanking/session without token -> 401', async () =>
    expectStatus(
      await call('/api/enablebanking/session', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ aspspName: 'Bank Millennium', aspspCountry: 'PL', orgId: 'victim-org' }),
      }),
      401,
    ),
  )
  await check('GET /api/enablebanking/institutions without token -> 401', async () =>
    expectStatus(await call('/api/enablebanking/institutions?country=PL'), 401),
  )
  await check('GET /api/enablebanking/callback with forged state -> 400', async () =>
    expectStatus(await call(`/api/enablebanking/callback?code=fake-code&state=${forgedState}`), 400),
  )

  const failedCount = results.filter((ok) => !ok).length
  console.log(failedCount === 0 ? 'all checks passed' : `${failedCount} check(s) failed`)
  if (failedCount > 0) die(`${failedCount} production check(s) failed`)
}

// ---------- main ----------

const commands = {
  'project-create': projectCreate,
  'project-show': projectShow,
  domains,
  'env-sync': envSync,
  'env-list': envList,
  deployments,
  wait,
  'redeploy-prod': redeployProd,
  'smoke-prod': smokeProd,
}

try {
  const [command, ...rest] = process.argv.slice(2)
  if (!command || !Object.hasOwn(commands, command)) {
    die(`usage: node scripts/vercel.mjs <${Object.keys(commands).join('|')}> [--flags]`)
  }
  await commands[command](parseFlags(rest))
} catch (e) {
  console.error(e instanceof Fatal ? `ERROR: ${e.message}` : e)
  process.exitCode = 1
}
```

- [ ] **Step 12: [USER] Make Vercel accept the commits it deploys (Hobby commit-author rule)**

On the Hobby plan, Vercel builds a Git commit only if its **author** is the owner of the Hobby team, which Vercel checks against the owner's GitHub *Login Connection* (https://vercel.com/docs/deployments/troubleshoot-project-collaboration, "Hobby teams"). Otherwise the deployment ends `BLOCKED` with `seatBlock.blockCode` `COMMIT_AUTHOR_REQUIRED`: no preview per PR, and criterion 1 ("push to `master` deploys") fails. Vercel checks the deployed commit only: the PR head commit for a preview, and the merge commit (authored by the GitHub account that runs `gh pr merge`) for production. The S00 commits so far are authored by `rekrutacjagd523-prog`, while `gh` (and so every merge) acts as `remit-agency`. Fix the root cause now, before Task A4 makes the last commit, instead of falling back to `redeploy-prod` later.

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
echo "gh (merges) acts as: $(gh api user --jq .login)"
echo "local commit author: $(git config user.name)"
git log --format='%an' origin/master..HEAD | sort | uniq -c
```

Expected: `gh (merges) acts as: remit-agency`, `local commit author: rekrutacjagd523-prog`, and the branch commits counted under `rekrutacjagd523-prog`.

Send:

> Vercel на безкоштовному плані Hobby деплоїть коміт лише тоді, коли його автор — власник команди, тобто GitHub-акаунт, підключений до твого Vercel-акаунта. Інакше деплой отримує статус `BLOCKED`, і пункт «пуш у master деплоїть на production» не виконується.
> 1. Відкрий https://vercel.com/account/settings/authentication → розділ *Login Connections*.
> 2. Перевір, що там підключено GitHub-акаунт **`remit-agency`** (він володіє репозиторієм і мерджить PR). Якщо підключено інший акаунт або жодного, підключи GitHub і увійди як `remit-agency`.
> 3. Напиши, який GitHub-акаунт тепер підключено.
> Після цього я вкажу `remit-agency` (зі службовою noreply-адресою GitHub) автором комітів у цьому репозиторії, щоб Vercel приймав коміти, які деплоїть. На інші репозиторії це не впливає.

When the user confirms `remit-agency`, set this repository's commit author (repo-local `.git/config` only, never `--global`):

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
LOGIN="$(gh api user --jq .login)"; GH_ID="$(gh api user --jq .id)"
git config user.name "$LOGIN"
git config user.email "${GH_ID}+${LOGIN}@users.noreply.github.com"
git config user.name; git config user.email | sed -E 's/^[0-9]+\+/<id>+/'
```

Expected: `remit-agency` and `<id>+remit-agency@users.noreply.github.com`. GitHub maps that noreply address to `remit-agency`, so Task A4's commit (the PR head) and every later commit are authored by the connected account, and the merge commits are too. Earlier commits keep their author. That is fine, because Vercel builds only the head commit of a push (previews of earlier draft-PR pushes may have been `BLOCKED`; they do not matter). Task E5 Step 1 checks the head commit's author.

If the user cannot connect `remit-agency` (for example, it is already linked to another Vercel account), stop and agree on another way with the user before Task A4 (for example, keeping the current author and merging with `gh pr merge --rebase`, so that the head of `master` keeps an author Vercel accepts). Do not continue to Task E5 with an author Vercel will block.

- [ ] **Step 13: Check the Enable Banking JWT against the live API (once the App ID is known)**

This proves, before the PR, that the App ID belongs to the uploaded key and that `lib/enablebanking/jwt.ts` (Task A2) builds claims Enable Banking accepts. It makes one read-only call, `GET https://api.enablebanking.com/application` ("Get application associated with provided JWT key ID" in the API reference). The App ID and the key go from `.secrets/` straight into the process environment; nothing secret is printed. Skip this step (and repeat it in Task E6 Step 4) if Step 8 has no App ID yet.

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
node --no-warnings --conditions=react-server --input-type=module - <<'EOF'
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
const idFile = '.secrets/enablebanking-app-id.txt'
const fromLocal = existsSync('.env.local') ? (readFileSync('.env.local', 'utf8').match(/^ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID=(.+)$/m) ?? [])[1] : undefined
const appId = (fromLocal ?? (existsSync(idFile) ? readFileSync(idFile, 'utf8') : '')).trim()
if (!appId) {
  console.log('SKIP: no Enable Banking App ID yet (Step 8)')
} else {
  process.env.ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID = appId
  process.env.ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY = readFileSync('.secrets/enablebanking-private.key', 'utf8').replace(/\r/g, '')
  const { createJwt } = await import('./lib/enablebanking/jwt.ts')
  const res = await fetch('https://api.enablebanking.com/application', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${await createJwt()}` } })
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
  console.log(`GET /application: HTTP ${res.status}`)
  if (res.ok) console.log(`environment=${body.environment} name=${body.name} active=${body.active} redirect_urls=${(body.redirect_urls ?? []).length}`)
  else console.log(`error: ${body.message ?? body.error ?? '(no message)'}`)
  process.exitCode = res.ok ? 0 : 1
}
EOF
```

Expected: `GET /application: HTTP 200` and `environment=PRODUCTION name=<the app's name> active=true redirect_urls=<n>`. Then:
- `active=false`: the application is not activated yet (Enable Banking Control Panel, for example Restricted Mode by linking the user's own account). Tell the user now; Task E6 Step 2 repeats the reminder, and the bank page in criterion 6 does not open otherwise.
- `HTTP 403` with `error: Application does not exist`: Enable Banking does not know this App ID (this exact answer came back for a random UUID and a throwaway key when the script was tried on 2026-10-22). Re-check Step 8 with the user.
- Any other HTTP 401/403: either the App ID does not belong to `enablebanking-public.crt` (re-check Step 8 with the user), or Enable Banking rejects the JWT claims. Compare `lib/enablebanking/jwt.ts` with "JWT format and signature" in https://enablebanking.com/docs/api/reference/ (header `typ`/`alg`/`kid`, body `iss: "enablebanking.com"`, `aud: "api.enablebanking.com"`, TTL ≤ 24 h) and raise it with the user. Fix `jwt.ts` together with its check (Task A2 Steps 5–7), commit on `s00-platform` with the `Co-Authored-By` trailer **before Task A4**, and re-run this step until it prints HTTP 200. That way the fix ships in the S00 PR instead of a second PR after branch protection.

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `CLAUDE.md` (full file)**

````markdown
@AGENTS.md

# CLAUDE.md — FinFlow Project Rules

## Project Overview
**FinFlow** — SaaS cash-flow and management-accounting app for small/medium businesses (2–50 people). Target market: Poland (PL/EN/UA interface planned in S03, base currency PLN).
Working today: accounts, income/expense/transfer transactions, Cash Flow, P&L, Balance, payment calendar, CSV export, Enable Banking bank connection (beta).
Roadmap: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-finflow-roadmap.md`. Sub-project specs: `docs/superpowers/specs/`, plans: `docs/superpowers/plans/`, change log: `PROGRESS.md`.

## Tech Stack
- **Framework:** Next.js 16 (App Router; Turbopack for `next dev` and `next build`), React 19.2, TypeScript 5 (strict)
- **Proxy:** `proxy.ts` — the Next 16 name for middleware (Node.js runtime). It only checks that the `__session` cookie exists; S01 replaces it with a verified server session.
- **Client data:** Firebase JS SDK 12 (Auth + Firestore), used only from `lib/firebase/` and `lib/hooks/`
- **Server data:** `firebase-admin`, used ONLY inside `lib/server/` (each file starts with `import 'server-only'`); entry point `getAdmin()` in `lib/server/firebase-admin.ts`. Firestore uses `preferRest: true` in production only (faster serverless cold start); on the emulator it uses gRPC, because REST would need Application Default Credentials
- **UI:** shadcn/ui (`base-nova` style on `@base-ui/react`), Recharts, Tailwind CSS 4, lucide-react, next-themes, sonner
- **Banking:** Enable Banking (AIS/PSD2) — routes `app/api/enablebanking/*`, `lib/enablebanking/jwt.ts` (server-only JWT), `lib/enablebanking/client.ts` (`ebFetch` adds the Firebase ID token for client components)
- **Payments:** Stripe is NOT wired yet (`lib/stripe.ts` is unused; billing arrives in S17)
- **Tooling:** npm, Node.js 24 (`engines.node` = `24.x`), ESLint 9 flat config (`eslint.config.mjs` + `eslint-config-next` 16), Vitest, `@firebase/rules-unit-testing`, Playwright

## Hosting & Infrastructure
- **Hosting:** Vercel — team `biuro-3838s-projects` (Hobby), project `finflow`, Git integration with GitHub `remit-agency/finflow`. Push to `master` → production; every PR → preview deployment. Hobby builds a commit only if its author is the GitHub account connected to the Vercel owner (`remit-agency`); this repo's local git author is set to it, otherwise deployments end `BLOCKED`.
- **Function region:** `fra1` (Frankfurt), pinned in `vercel.json` (`{ "regions": ["fra1"] }`). Node.js 24.x.
- **Firebase:** project `finflow-e94b5`, Firestore location `eur3` (EU multi-region). Rules `firestore.rules` (deny by default), `storage.rules` (deny all — Storage is unused), indexes `firestore.indexes.json`, all wired in `firebase.json`; default project in `.firebaserc`.
  Deploy rules + indexes: `firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,firestore:indexes --project finflow-e94b5 --account "$FIREBASE_ACCOUNT"` (the Firebase CLI account is `FIREBASE_ACCOUNT` in the gitignored `.env`). Add `,storage` only once a default Storage bucket exists: without one the CLI aborts with `Firebase Storage has not been set up`.
- **Health check:** `GET /api/health` → `{ ok, region, firestore: 'ok' }` (admin read of `_meta/health`; no user data).
- **CI:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` on every PR and every push to `master`: lint → typecheck → unit → rules (emulator) → Playwright e2e (emulators) → build. `master` is protected: merge only via PR with green CI.
- **Cloudflare Pages / OpenNext / wrangler were removed in S00.** Do not reintroduce `getCloudflareContext`, webpack aliases for `@firebase/*`, a custom `webpack()` config (it fails Turbopack builds) or post-build patch scripts.

## Environment Variables
Names are listed in `.env.example` (committed, names only). Local values live in `.env.local` (gitignored). Never commit, log or paste values.

| Variable | Kind (Vercel) | Read by |
|---|---|---|
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY`, `_AUTH_DOMAIN`, `_PROJECT_ID`, `_STORAGE_BUCKET`, `_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID`, `_APP_ID`, `_MEASUREMENT_ID` | plain | `lib/firebase/firebase.ts` |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` | plain | Enable Banking redirect URL, `app/robots.ts`, `app/sitemap.ts` |
| `ENABLEBANKING_APP_ID`, `ENABLEBANKING_PRIVATE_KEY` | sensitive | `lib/enablebanking/jwt.ts` |
| `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` (base64 of the service-account JSON) | sensitive | `lib/server/firebase-admin.ts` |
| `EB_STATE_SECRET` (32 random bytes, base64) | sensitive | `lib/server/eb-state.ts` |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1`, `FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST`, `FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST` | local / CI only | emulator wiring |

Secret sources on the dev machine (gitignored): `.secrets/` (Enable Banking key + certificate, Firebase service-account JSON) and `.env` (tooling: `VERCEL_TOKEN`, `VERCEL_TEAM_ID`, `FIREBASE_ACCOUNT`).

## Commands
| Command | What it does |
|---|---|
| `npm run dev` | Next dev server (Turbopack; output in `.next/dev`; also refreshes `AGENTS.md`) |
| `npm run build` / `npm start` | production build / serve it |
| `npm run lint` | `eslint .` — `next build` no longer lints, so run it before every commit |
| `npm run typecheck` | `tsc --noEmit` |
| `npm run test:unit` | Vitest, `tests/unit/**/*.test.ts` (`vitest.config.ts`; `server-only` is stubbed) |
| `npm run test:rules` | Firestore rules tests on the emulator, `tests/rules/**/*.test.ts` (`vitest.rules.config.ts`, project `demo-finflow`) |
| `npm run test:e2e` | Playwright, `tests/e2e/**/*.spec.ts`; the smoke flow needs the Auth + Firestore emulators — see `.github/workflows/ci.yml` for the exact command and env |

The Firebase emulators need Java 21. No system-wide JDK is installed on the dev machine, so rules tests and e2e-with-emulators run in CI. To run them locally, either install Temurin 21 (`winget install EclipseAdoptium.Temurin.21.JDK`) and open a new terminal, or use the portable JRE in `~/.jre/temurin-21` (only if the user agreed to unpack it during S00) by prefixing the command with `PATH="$HOME/.jre/temurin-21/bin:$PATH"` in Git Bash.

## Folder Structure
```
app/
  (auth)/login, register          ← auth pages (client components)
  (app)/[orgSlug]/                ← signed-in app; orgSlug is the Firestore orgId
    transactions/  analytics/{cashflow,pl,balance,calendar}/  settings/
  api/health/route.ts             ← health check (firebase-admin)
  api/enablebanking/{institutions,session,callback,accounts}/route.ts
  page.tsx (landing), pricing/, layout.tsx, globals.css, robots.ts, sitemap.ts
components/
  ui/            ← shadcn generated files (DO NOT EDIT by hand)
  charts/ forms/ transactions/ accounts/ settings/ landing/  ThemeToggle.tsx
lib/
  firebase/      ← client SDK init (firebase.ts) + Firestore helpers — browser only
  hooks/         ← useAuth, useOrg, useAccounts, useCategories, useTransactions
  server/        ← server-only: firebase-admin.ts, auth.ts, http-error.ts, eb-state.ts
  enablebanking/ ← jwt.ts (server-only), client.ts (ebFetch)
  utils/         ← analytics, currency, date, export (CSV)
types/           ← shared TS types (Organization, Account, Transaction, …)
tests/
  unit/          ← Vitest
  rules/         ← Firestore security-rules tests (emulator)
  e2e/           ← Playwright
proxy.ts         ← Next 16 proxy (ex-middleware)
firebase.json  .firebaserc  firestore.rules  firestore.indexes.json  storage.rules
vercel.json  .github/workflows/ci.yml  .env.example  AGENTS.md (managed by next dev)
docs/            ← research, specs, plans
```

## Firestore Data Model (S00)
- `organizations/{orgId}`: `{ name, currency, ownerId, plan, trialEndsAt, createdAt, members: { [uid]: { role: 'owner' } } }`
  - subcollections: `accounts`, `categories`, `transactions`, `bankConnections`
- `_meta/{docId}` — server-only (for example `_meta/health`); clients are denied by the rules
- Membership lookup on the client: `where('members.<uid>.role', '!=', null)` in `useOrg`. S02 moves membership into its own documents.

## Coding Conventions
- TypeScript strict mode always
- Server Components by default, `'use client'` only when needed (forms, hooks, charts)
- Next 16: `params`, `searchParams`, `cookies()`, `headers()` are async — always `await` them (or React `use()` in client components)
- Client Firebase calls only in `/lib/firebase/` and `/lib/hooks/` (legacy exception: `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/settings/page.tsx` still calls `updateDoc`/`deleteDoc` directly)
- Server-side Firestore/Auth only through `getAdmin()` from `lib/server/`; route handlers that use it `export const runtime = 'nodejs'`; map errors with `HttpError` + `errorResponse()`
- Browser → API calls that need a user send `Authorization: Bearer <Firebase ID token>` (`ebFetch`); the route calls `requireUserFromBearer()` and `requireOrgMember()`
- `cn()` from `lib/utils` for className merging
- All dates: `Date` objects in code, Firestore `Timestamp` in DB
- Currency formatting: `Intl.NumberFormat` via `formatCurrency(amount, currency)`
- Before committing: `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test:unit`

## Design System

### Mood: Minimal + Trust (фінанси = точність)
### Style: Linear-inspired — dark sidebar, white content, one accent
- Marketing pages (`/`, `/pricing`): light + dark theme via `next-themes` (default dark, `ThemeToggle`)

### Color Tokens
```css
--bg-app: #0f0f0f          /* app background */
--bg-sidebar: #111111       /* sidebar */
--bg-card: #ffffff          /* content cards */
--bg-hover: #f9fafb         /* row hover */
--accent: #00c896           /* primary action (teal-green) */
--income: #22c55e           /* green-500 */
--expense: #ef4444          /* red-500 */
--transfer: #6366f1         /* indigo-500 */
--text-primary: #111111
--text-muted: #6b7280
--border: #e5e7eb
```

### Typography
- Fonts: `Unbounded` (display/headings — CSS var `--font-display`, class `font-display`) + `Manrope` (body — `--font-body`), loaded with `next/font/google` in `app/layout.tsx` (latin + cyrillic)
- Headings: 600 weight
- Body: 400, line-height 1.6
- Numbers/data: `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums`

### Spacing (4px base)
- Card padding: 16–24px
- Section gap: 24px
- Form gap: 16px

### Border Radius
- Cards: 12px
- Buttons: 8px
- Inputs: 6px

### Components
- Buttons: ONE primary CTA per section (accent color), rest ghost/outline
- Forms: inline validation, disabled submit until valid
- Tables: zebra striping via `--bg-hover`, sticky header
- Charts: Recharts with custom tooltips matching design system

## Banned Copy Phrases
- "seamless", "powerful", "robust", "leverage", "streamline"
- "game-changer", "revolutionary", "cutting-edge"
- "in today's fast-paced world"
- Generic CTAs like "Get Started" → use "Start free trial" or "Try 14 days free"
- No invented testimonials, customer counts or ratings; never advertise a feature that is not shipped

## What NOT to Touch
- `/components/ui/` — shadcn generated files, edit via CLI only
- `lib/firebase/firebase.ts` config — environment variables only, no hardcoded keys
- `.env`, `.env.local`, `.secrets/` — never commit, never copy values into code, docs or chat
- The managed block in `AGENTS.md` — written by `next dev`; commit it as is

## Freemium / Trial Logic
- Today: registration (`lib/firebase/firestore.ts`) stores `plan: 'trial'` and `trialEndsAt` = now + 14 days on the organization; nothing enforces the trial yet
- Clients cannot change `plan`, `trialEndsAt`, `ownerId` or `members` (`firestore.rules`)
- Planned (S17): Stripe billing in PLN, read-only after the trial, `/pricing` redirect

- [ ] **Step 2: Make sure no secret is anywhere in the branch history (the repo is public)**

The scan compares fingerprints of the real local secrets with `git log -p origin/master..HEAD`. It prints only the result.

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'
import fs from 'node:fs'
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process'
const range = 'origin/master..HEAD'
const history = execFileSync('git', ['log', '-p', range], { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 * 1024 })
const dot = Object.fromEntries(
  fs.readFileSync('.env', 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)
    .filter((l) => /^[A-Z0-9_]+=/.test(l))
    .map((l) => [l.slice(0, l.indexOf('=')), l.slice(l.indexOf('=') + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')]),
)
const secondLine = (pem) => String(pem ?? '').replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n')[1]
const saFile = fs.readdirSync('.secrets').find((f) => /^finflow-e94b5-firebase-adminsdk-.+\.json$/.test(f))
const saRaw = saFile ? fs.readFileSync(`.secrets/${saFile}`, 'utf8') : ''
let sa = {}
try { sa = JSON.parse(saRaw) } catch { console.log('warning: SA JSON unreadable, skipping its fingerprints') }
const fingerprints = {
  VERCEL_TOKEN: dot.VERCEL_TOKEN,
  CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: dot.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN,
  'service account private_key_id': sa.private_key_id,
  'service account private key': secondLine(sa.private_key),
  'service account base64': Buffer.from(saRaw, 'utf8').toString('base64').slice(1500, 1564),
  'Enable Banking private key': secondLine(fs.readFileSync('.secrets/enablebanking-private.key', 'utf8')),
}
const checked = Object.entries(fingerprints).filter(([, v]) => typeof v === 'string' && v.length >= 16)
const leaks = checked.filter(([, v]) => history.includes(v)).map(([k]) => k)
console.log(leaks.length > 0
  ? `LEAK in ${range}: ${leaks.join(', ')}`
  : `clean: ${checked.length} secret fingerprints, none found in ${range} (${history.length} chars)`)
if (leaks.length > 0) process.exitCode = 1
EOF
```

Expected: `clean: 6 secret fingerprints, none found in origin/master..HEAD (...)`. On `LEAK`, **do not push**. Rewrite the offending commit locally (e.g. `git rebase` with an edit of that commit), re-run this step, and tell the user which secret was involved so it can be rotated if it ever left the machine. Since Task E3 Step 6 pushes the branch early, check `git branch -r --contains <offending sha>`: if `origin/s00-platform` already contains it, the secret is public. Tell the user to rotate it right away, then force-push the rewritten `s00-platform` (`git push --force-with-lease origin s00-platform`, never to `master`).

cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }

(`wrangler.toml`, which used to hold these values, is deleted in Part A2, and `.env` has no `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`.) The commands below use `NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY` from the environment when it is set, and fall back to this file otherwise.

- [ ] **Step 3: Back up the currently released ruleset (read-only, for rollback)**

```bash
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { initializeApp, cert } from 'firebase-admin/app'
import { getSecurityRules } from 'firebase-admin/security-rules'

const saFiles = readdirSync('.secrets').filter((f) => /^finflow-e94b5-firebase-adminsdk-.+[.]json$/.test(f))
if (saFiles.length !== 1) throw new Error(`expected one service-account JSON in .secrets/, found ${saFiles.length}`)
const sa = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`.secrets/${saFiles[0]}`, 'utf8'))
const current = await getSecurityRules(initializeApp({ credential: cert(sa) })).getFirestoreRuleset()
writeFileSync('.secrets/firestore-ruleset-before-s00.json', JSON.stringify(current, null, 2))
console.log(`backed up ${current.name} (created ${current.createTime})`)
const source = current.source.map((f) => f.content).join('\n')
console.log(source.includes('timestamp.date(2026, 5, 7)') ? 'expired test-mode rules' : 'NOT the expected test-mode rules: read the backup before continuing')
EOF
```

Expected (as of 2026-10-22): `backed up 8ecea49c-f0f7-4858-9eae-7704a8702db3 (created Tue, 07 Apr 2026 08:12:27 GMT)` and `expired test-mode rules`. A different name means someone released rules since then. Read `.secrets/firestore-ruleset-before-s00.json` and tell the user before going on.

KEY="${NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY:-$(node -p "require('./.secrets/firebase-web-sdkconfig.json').apiKey")}"
OUT="$(mktemp)"
curl -s -o "$OUT" -w 'HTTP %{http_code}\n' "https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/finflow-e94b5/databases/(default)/documents/organizations?key=$KEY"
node -p "const e = JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync(process.argv[1], 'utf8')).error; e ? e.status + ': ' + e.message : 'NO ERROR (documents returned, not printed)'" "$OUT"
rm -f "$OUT"
```

Expected (2026-10-22): `HTTP 403` and `PERMISSION_DENIED: Missing or insufficient permissions.`. The expired test-mode rules already deny everyone, which is why Step 9 checks signed-in behaviour too. The response body is never printed, only its `error` fields.

- Only the three `index …` lines `FAIL` with `HTTP 400`: the indexes are still building (minutes). Re-run the same command after 5 minutes. Every run leaves one more org, and Step 10 removes them all.
- Rules lines fail in the first minute after the deploy: rules propagation (up to about 1 minute for new queries). Re-run once after 2 minutes.
- `FAIL sign up throwaway user: HTTP 400 OPERATION_NOT_ALLOWED`: the Email/Password provider is disabled. Report it to the user; the rules checks cannot run.

- [ ] **Step 10: Delete the verifier's leftover orgs**

```bash
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { initializeApp, cert } from 'firebase-admin/app'
import { getFirestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'

const saFiles = readdirSync('.secrets').filter((f) => /^finflow-e94b5-firebase-adminsdk-.+[.]json$/.test(f))
if (saFiles.length !== 1) throw new Error(`expected one service-account JSON in .secrets/, found ${saFiles.length}`)
const sa = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`.secrets/${saFiles[0]}`, 'utf8'))
const db = getFirestore(initializeApp({ credential: cert(sa) }))
const names = ['rules-check (safe to delete)', 'rules-check renamed (safe to delete)']
const found = await db.collection('organizations').where('name', 'in', names).get()
for (const d of found.docs) {
  await db.recursiveDelete(d.ref)
  console.log(`deleted ${d.ref.path}`)
}
const left = await db.collection('organizations').where('name', 'in', names).get()
console.log(`rules-check orgs left: ${left.size}`)
EOF
```

Expected: one `deleted organizations/<id>` per verifier run (matching the `LEFTOVER_ORG` lines), then `rules-check orgs left: 0`. `recursiveDelete` also removes the `accounts` doc the verifier created.

> 3. Натисни F12 → **Console**: там не має бути червоного `FirebaseError: Missing or insufficient permissions`.
>
> Відповідь: «ок» або встав червоний текст із консолі разом з адресою сторінки.

Expected reply: `ок`. Then:

const saFiles = readdirSync('.secrets').filter((f) => /^finflow-e94b5-firebase-adminsdk-.+[.]json$/.test(f))

const sa = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`.secrets/${saFiles[0]}`, 'utf8'))

EOF

### Task 28: Branch protection on `master` (E7)

**Files:** none (GitHub settings via `gh api`).

`remit-agency` is a GitHub **user** account and the repo is **public**, so classic branch protection is available. `restrictions` must be `null` (push restrictions exist only for org repos). There is a single maintainer, so the required approvals are `0`: a PR is mandatory, but nobody has to approve their own work. `enforce_admins: true` makes the rule bind the admin too, which is what the spec's "merge only via PR with green CI" means. The required check is the Actions job `ci`; the GitHub Actions app id is `15368` (`gh api apps/github-actions`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Confirm the check-run name on the current `master` commit**

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
git fetch origin
gh api "repos/remit-agency/finflow/commits/$(git rev-parse origin/master)/check-runs" --jq '.check_runs[] | "\(.name)\t\(.app.slug)\t\(.app.id)\t\(.conclusion)"'
```

Expected: a line `ci	github-actions	15368	success` (Vercel lines may also appear). If the name is not `ci`, someone renamed the job or added a `name:`. Restore `jobs.ci` without `name:` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` through a PR before going on.

- [ ] **Step 2: Turn on protection**

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
gh api -X PUT repos/remit-agency/finflow/branches/master/protection -H "Accept: application/vnd.github+json" --input - --jq '.url' <<'EOF'
{
  "required_status_checks": {
    "strict": true,
    "checks": [{ "context": "ci", "app_id": 15368 }]
  },
  "enforce_admins": true,
  "required_pull_request_reviews": {
    "required_approving_review_count": 0,
    "dismiss_stale_reviews": false,
    "require_code_owner_reviews": false
  },
  "restrictions": null,
  "required_linear_history": false,
  "allow_force_pushes": false,
  "allow_deletions": false,
  "required_conversation_resolution": false
}
EOF
```

Expected: `https://api.github.com/repos/remit-agency/finflow/branches/master/protection`. A `422 Validation Failed` usually means a field name was mistyped. Compare with https://docs.github.com/rest/branches/branch-protection#update-branch-protection.

- [ ] **Step 3: Read it back**

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
gh api repos/remit-agency/finflow/branches/master/protection --jq '{strict: .required_status_checks.strict, checks: [.required_status_checks.checks[].context], enforce_admins: .enforce_admins.enabled, approvals: .required_pull_request_reviews.required_approving_review_count, force_pushes: .allow_force_pushes.enabled, deletions: .allow_deletions.enabled}'
gh api repos/remit-agency/finflow/branches/master --jq '.protected'
```

Expected: `{"approvals":0,"checks":["ci"],"deletions":false,"enforce_admins":true,"force_pushes":false,"strict":true}` (key order and spacing may differ), then `true`.

The read-back of Step 3 is the proof that `master` is protected (criterion 3). Step 4 is an optional extra check of the same thing.

- [ ] **Step 4 (optional): Probe that a direct push is rejected**

Run it only if Step 3 printed exactly `"checks":["ci"]`, `"enforce_admins":true`, `"strict":true` and `protected` `true`. Otherwise do not probe: fix Steps 2–3 first, because a probe against an unprotected `master` would land a commit there. `git commit-tree` builds a commit object without moving any branch, so nothing local needs resetting. The message carries the `Co-Authored-By` trailer like every other commit.

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
PROBE="$(git commit-tree "origin/master^{tree}" -p origin/master -m "probe: branch protection (never merged)" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>")"
git push origin "$PROBE:refs/heads/master"; echo "push-exit=$?"
```

Expected: `remote: error: GH006: Protected branch update failed for refs/heads/master.`, lines saying changes must be made through a pull request and that check `ci` is expected, then `! [remote rejected] ... -> master (protected branch hook declined)` and `push-exit=1`. If the push **succeeds** despite the Step 3 read-back, stop and tell the user right away: an empty commit (same tree as before) is now on `master` outside a PR and has started a production deployment. Do not try to remove it with a force push. Re-check Steps 2–3 with the user.

No commit in this task.

EOF

import fs from 'node:fs'
const env = Object.fromEntries(
  fs.readFileSync('.env', 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)
    .filter((l) => /^[A-Z0-9_]+=/.test(l))
    .map((l) => [l.slice(0, l.indexOf('=')), l.slice(l.indexOf('=') + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')]),
)
const ACC = env.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID
const PROJECT = 'finflow1'
const cf = async (method, p) =>
  (await fetch(`https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/${p}`, { method, headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}` } })).json()
let del = await cf('DELETE', `accounts/${ACC}/pages/projects/${PROJECT}`)
if (!del.success) {
  console.log(`direct delete failed: ${JSON.stringify(del.errors)}; deleting deployments first`)
  for (let pass = 1; pass <= 20; pass++) {
    const list = await cf('GET', `accounts/${ACC}/pages/projects/${PROJECT}/deployments?per_page=25`)
    const ids = (list.result ?? []).map((d) => d.id)
    if (ids.length === 0) break
    let removed = 0
    for (const id of ids) {
      const r = await cf('DELETE', `accounts/${ACC}/pages/projects/${PROJECT}/deployments/${id}?force=true`)
      if (r.success) removed++
    }
    console.log(`pass ${pass}: removed ${removed}/${ids.length} deployments`)
    if (removed === 0) break
  }
  del = await cf('DELETE', `accounts/${ACC}/pages/projects/${PROJECT}`)
}
console.log(`delete ${PROJECT}: success=${del.success}${del.success ? '' : ` ${JSON.stringify(del.errors)}`}`)
const after = await cf('GET', `accounts/${ACC}/pages/projects/${PROJECT}`)
console.log(after.success ? `${PROJECT} STILL EXISTS` : `${PROJECT} is gone (${after.errors?.[0]?.code} ${after.errors?.[0]?.message})`)
if (!del.success || after.success) process.exitCode = 1
EOF
```

Expected: `delete finflow1: success=true`, then `finflow1 is gone (8000007 Project not found. The specified project name does not match any of your existing projects.)`. Code 8000007 for a missing project was verified on 2026-10-22. The fallback loop only runs if Cloudflare refuses the direct delete, for example over the >100-deployments limit described in its Pages known issues. On a persistent failure, **[USER]**: Cloudflare dashboard → *Workers & Pages* → `finflow1` → *Settings* → *Delete project*.

- [ ] **Step 5: Revoke the Cloudflare API token (self-revoke attempt)**

```sh
cd /f/AI/finflow || exit 1
[ "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "F:/AI/finflow" ] || { echo WRONG REPO; exit 1; }
node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'
import fs from 'node:fs'
const env = Object.fromEntries(
  fs.readFileSync('.env', 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)
    .filter((l) => /^[A-Z0-9_]+=/.test(l))
    .map((l) => [l.slice(0, l.indexOf('=')), l.slice(l.indexOf('=') + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')]),
)
const ACC = env.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID
const cf = async (method, p) =>
  (await fetch(`https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/${p}`, { method, headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN}` } })).json()
const before = await cf('GET', `accounts/${ACC}/tokens/verify`)
if (!before.success) {
  console.log(`token already invalid: ${before.errors?.[0]?.code} ${before.errors?.[0]?.message}`)
} else {
  const r = await cf('DELETE', `accounts/${ACC}/tokens/${before.result.id}`)
  console.log(`self-revoke: success=${r.success}${r.success ? '' : ` ${JSON.stringify(r.errors)}`}`)
  const after = await cf('GET', `accounts/${ACC}/tokens/verify`)
  console.log(after.success
    ? `token STILL ACTIVE (status=${after.result?.status}); revoke it in the dashboard (Step 6)`
    : `token revoked (${after.errors?.[0]?.code} ${after.errors?.[0]?.message})`)
}
EOF
```

Expected, one of two outcomes:
- `self-revoke: success=true` and `token revoked (1000 Invalid API Token)`: skip Step 6.
- `self-revoke: success=false [<authorization error>]` and `token STILL ACTIVE`: the token lacks "Account API Tokens Write" and cannot delete itself, so do Step 6.
